function studentEntryDate(student){
  const raw=student?.entryDate||student?.statusDate||student?.moveInDate||student?.admissionDate||student?.enrollmentDate||"";
  const value=String(raw||"").slice(0,10);
  return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)?value:"";
}
function studentLinkedRecordDate(item){
  const raw=item?.date||item?.day||item?.activityDate||item?.attendanceDate||item?.submittedAt||item?.finalSubmittedAt||item?.startDate||item?.endDate||item?.recordDate||item?.createdAt||"";
  const value=String(raw||"").slice(0,10);
  return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(value)?value:"";
}
function studentRecordWithinEnrollment(item,student){
  const entry=studentEntryDate(student);
  if(!entry||!/전입/.test(String(student?.status||student?.enrollmentStatus||student?.enrollmentReason||"")))return true;
  const recordDate=studentLinkedRecordDate(item);
  return !recordDate||recordDate>=entry;
}
function normalizedVolunteerTitle(value){
  const text=String(value||"").replace(/^\s*\((?:자율[·ㆍ.\s-]*자치(?:활동)?|진로[·ㆍ.\s-]*활동|동아리[·ㆍ.\s-]*활동)\)\s*/,"").replace(/\s/g,"");
  if(/중식/.test(text)&&/도우미/.test(text))return "중식도우미";
  if(/석식/.test(text)&&/도우미/.test(text))return "석식도우미";
  return text.replace(/급식/g,"");
}
function volunteerIdentity(item){
  return [String(item?.date||item?.endDate||item?.startDate||""),normalizedVolunteerTitle(item?.title||item?.type||"")].join("|");
}
function isVolunteerActivity(item){
  return /봉사|도우미|필사집|교과도우미|수업\s*지원/.test(`${item?.area||""} ${item?.type||""} ${item?.title||""} ${item?.role||""}`);
}
function isTrueClubActivity(item){
  const text=`${item?.area||""} ${item?.type||""} ${item?.title||""} ${item?.role||""}`;
  return /동아리/.test(text)&&!isVolunteerActivity(item)&&!/\(동아리활동\)/.test(text);
}

function reportTimestampValue(report){
  const raw=String(report?.finalSubmittedAt||report?.submittedAt||report?.date||'').trim();
  const serial=Number(raw);
  if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000)return Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+serial*86400000;
  const parsed=Date.parse(raw.replace(/오전/g,'AM').replace(/오후/g,'PM'));
  return Number.isFinite(parsed)?parsed:0;
}
function canonicalReportKey(report){
  const pid=unifiedProgramId(reportProgramId(report));
  if(pid)return `id:${pid}`;
  const name=[report?.reportGroup,report?.programName,report?.programTitle,report?.activityTitle,report?.title]
    .map(recordProgramMatchName).find(Boolean)||'';
  return name?`name:${name}`:`report:${String(report?.id||report?.reportId||'')}`;
}
function studentCanonicalReports(student){
  const rows=(readonlyCache?.programReports||readonlyCache?.reports||[])
    .filter(r=>r&&r.submitted!==false&&studentMatches(r,student)&&studentRecordWithinEnrollment(r,student))
    .slice().sort((a,b)=>reportTimestampValue(b)-reportTimestampValue(a)||Number(b?.submissionCount||1)-Number(a?.submissionCount||1));
  const map=new Map();
  for(const row of rows){const key=canonicalReportKey(row);if(!map.has(key))map.set(key,row);}
  return [...map.values()].sort((a,b)=>reportTimestampValue(a)-reportTimestampValue(b)||String(a.reportGroup||a.programName||'').localeCompare(String(b.reportGroup||b.programName||''),'ko'));
}

function afterSchoolProgramGroups() {
  const connected = (readonlyCache?.programs || []).map(programWithOverride).filter((program) => program.kind === "after").map((program) => ({...program, reportRequired:false}));
  const rows = (readonlyCache?.afterSchoolAttendance || []).slice();
  const groups = new Map();
  rows.forEach((row) => {
    const type = String(row.type || "방과후 프로그램").trim() || "방과후 프로그램";
    const time = String(row.time || row.period || "").trim();
    const key = [row.date || "", type, time].join("|");
    if (!groups.has(key)) groups.set(key, { key, id: `after-${encodeURIComponent(key)}`, date: row.date || "", title: type, actualTitle: type, recordTitle: type, time: time || "시간 미등록", place: row.detail || "운영정보 확인", teacher: "", records: [], kind: "after", reportRequired: false, affectsAttendance: /오후자습/.test(type), source:"30_야자출결_정규화" });
    groups.get(key).records.push(row);
  });
  const derived = [...groups.values()].map((program) => {
    const seen = new Set();
    program.students = program.records.filter((row) => {
      const key = attendanceStudentKey(row);
      if (seen.has(key)) return false;
      seen.add(key);
      return true;
    });
    program.attendedCount = program.students.filter(nightRecordIsAttendance).length;
    return program;
  });
  const connectedKeys = new Set(connected.map((program)=>`${program.date}|${program.title}|${program.time}`));
  return [...connected, ...derived.filter((program)=>!connectedKeys.has(`${program.date}|${program.title}|${program.time}`))].sort((a,b) => String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)) || String(a.title).localeCompare(String(b.title)));
}

function groupedAfterSchoolCourses(items) {
  const groups=new Map();

function groupedAfterSchoolCourses(items) {
  const groups=new Map();
  (items||[]).filter(p=>p&&p.kind==="after").forEach(program=>{
    const courseId=String(program.courseId||program.programId||program.id||"").replace(/^after-session-/,"");
    const key=courseId||`${program.actualTitle||program.title}|${program.type||""}`;
    if(!groups.has(key)) groups.set(key,{...program,id:`after-course-${key}`,sessionIds:[],dates:[],students:[],attendedCount:0,sessionCount:0});
    const group=groups.get(key);
    if(!program.isCourseMaster) group.sessionIds.push(program.sessionId||program.id);
    if(program.date) group.dates.push(program.date);
    if(program.endDate) group.dates.push(program.endDate);
    if(!program.isCourseMaster) group.sessionCount+=1;
    group.affectsAttendance=Boolean(group.affectsAttendance||programHasNightStudyImpact(program));
    if((group.dates||[]).length&&(group.dates||[]).every(isWednesdayKey)) group.affectsAttendance=false;
    const studentMap=new Map((group.students||[]).map(st=>[String(st.studentId||st.studentNo||st.number),st]));
    (program.students||[]).forEach(st=>{const sk=String(st.studentId||st.studentNo||st.number);if(sk&&!studentMap.has(sk))studentMap.set(sk,st);});
    group.students=[...studentMap.values()];
  });
  return [...groups.values()].map(group=>{
    const dates=[...new Set(group.dates)].sort();
    const first=dates[0]||group.date||"", last=dates.at(-1)||first;
    group.date=first;
    group.endDate=last;
    group.scheduleText=first&&last&&first!==last?`${first} ~ ${last}`:first;
    group.time=group.type==="여름방학"||/방학/.test(`${group.type||""} ${group.afterType||""}`)?group.time:(group.time||"시간 확인");
    group.attendedCount=group.students.filter(nightRecordIsAttendance).length;
    group.sessionNo=`${group.sessionCount}차시`;
    group.recordTitle="생활기록부 기록 대상 아님";
    return group;
  }).sort((a,b)=>String(b.date||"").localeCompare(String(a.date||""))||String(a.title||"").localeCompare(String(b.title||"")));
}

function programWithOverride(program) {
  if (!program || typeof program !== "object") return program;
  const override = state.programOverrides?.[program.id] || {};
  const merged = {...program, ...override, students: program.students || []};
  // 보고서 폼 URL은 프로그램별 로컬 override가 아니라 00_UEP설정의 공통값이 원본입니다.
  // 과거 로컬 빈값/구버전 override가 공통 URL을 가리지 않도록 화면 직전에도 표준 설정값을 우선 적용합니다.
  const uepSettings = readonlyCache?.uepSettings || {};
  if (program.kind === "selected") merged.reportFormUrl = String(uepSettings.SELECTED_ACTIVITY_REPORT_FORM_URL || program.reportFormUrl || "").trim();
  if (program.kind === "common") merged.reportFormUrl = String(uepSettings.COMMON_ACTIVITY_REPORT_FORM_URL || program.reportFormUrl || "").trim();
  return merged;
}
function programAreaKind(program) {
  if (program?.kind === "after" || /방과후/.test(String(program?.type || ""))) return "after";
  const text = `${program?.type || ""} ${program?.recordTitle || ""} ${program?.actualTitle || program?.title || ""}`;
  if (/진로/.test(text)) return "career";
  return "autonomy";
}
function programAreaLabel(program) {
  const kind = programAreaKind(program);
  if (kind === "career") return "진로";
  if (kind === "after") {
    const text = `${program?.afterType || ""} ${program?.type || ""} ${program?.actualTitle || program?.title || ""}`;
    if (/야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)) return "야간심화수업";
    return "방과후";
  }
  return "자율";
}

function programMonthKey(program) {
  const direct=String(program?.date||"").match(/^(\d{4}-\d{2})/);
  if(direct) return direct[1];
  const fallback=String(program?.scheduleText||program?.originalSchedule||"");
  const year=fallback.match(/(20\d{2})/)?.[1]||String(new Date().getFullYear());
  const month=fallback.match(/(?:^|\D)(1[0-2]|0?[1-9])\s*월/)?.[1];
  return month?`${year}-${String(month).padStart(2,"0")}`:"";
}

}

let workBoardMode="home";

function workBoardData(type){
  if(type==="notice"){
    if(!Array.isArray(state.workNotices))state.workNotices=[];
    return state.workNotices;
  }
  if(type==="deadline"){
    if(!Array.isArray(state.schoolDeadlines))state.schoolDeadlines=[];
    return state.schoolDeadlines;
  }
  return (state.tasks||[]).filter(task=>task?.project!=="UEP"&&(!task.owner||task.owner===currentWorkAudienceContext().name));
}

function alertProgramIsRecordTarget(program){
  if(!program||program.kind==="after"||program.kind==="common"||program.reportRequired===false)return false;
  const label=`${program.kind||""} ${program.activityType||""} ${program.programType||""} ${program.type||""} ${program.title||""}`;
  return /선택|자율|진로|챌린지|환경개선/.test(label)||program.kind==="selected"||program.kind==="general";
}

function timetableClassColorKey(room){
  const match=String(room||"").match(/(?:^|\b)([123])-?(\d{1,2})(?:\b|$)/);
  if(!match)return "";
  return `${match[1]}-${Number(match[2])}`;
}
function timetableClassColorClass(room){
  const key=timetableClassColorKey(room);
  if(!key)return "";
  let hash=0; for(const ch of key)hash=(hash*31+ch.charCodeAt(0))>>>0;
  return `class-color-${hash%9}`;
}


let dashboardTimetableMode="";
let studentTimetableSelectedId="";
function dashboardTimetableAutoMode(){
  if(dashboardTimetableMode)return dashboardTimetableMode;
  const now=new Date(),minute=now.getHours()*60+now.getMinutes();
  const end=(now.getDay()===3)?(14*60+30):(16*60+30);
  return minute>=end?"night":"day";
}

function dashboardAfterProgramsForDay(dayKey){

    const isAfter=/방과후|야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)||String(program.kind||"").toLowerCase()==="after";
    const pday=String(program.date||program.sessionDate||program.operationDate||"").slice(0,10);

function dashboardProgramNightSlot(program){
  const text=`${program.afterType||""} ${program.type||""} ${program.title||""} ${program.time||""}`;
  if(/야간심화/.test(text)){
    const m=String(program.time||"").match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/); const minute=m?Number(m[1])*60+Number(m[2]):0;
    return minute>=20*60+20?"야자2":"야자1";
  }
  return "오후자습";
}

    dates.forEach((date,i)=>{
      const key=dateKey(date),isWed=i===2,holiday=attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(key);
      if(holiday){cells.push(`<div class="dashboard-night-cell closed holiday"><b>미운영</b><small>공휴일</small></div>`);return;}
      if(isWed){cells.push(`<div class="dashboard-night-cell closed"><b>-</b><small>야간 미운영</small></div>`);return;}
      const rows=dashboardNightAttendanceRows(key,slot.key),students=new Set(rows.map(attendanceStudentKey).filter(Boolean));
      const programs=dashboardAfterProgramsForDay(key).filter(p=>dashboardProgramNightSlot(p)===slot.key);
      const programCards=programs.map(p=>`<button class="dashboard-night-program-chip" data-dashboard-night-program="${escapeHtml(p.id||p.programId||"")}" data-dashboard-night-program-day="${key}"><b>${/야간심화/.test(`${p.afterType||""} ${p.title||""}`)?"야간심화":"방과후"}</b><span>${escapeHtml(p.actualTitle||p.title||"프로그램")}</span></button>`).join("");
      cells.push(`<div class="dashboard-night-cell ${students.size||programs.length?'active':'empty'}"><button class="dashboard-night-roster" data-dashboard-night-day="${key}" data-dashboard-night-slot="${escapeHtml(slot.key)}"><b>${students.size?`${students.size}명`:'-'}</b><small>${students.size?'자습인원 보기':'자습 자료 없음'}</small></button>${programCards}</div>`);
    });
  });
  return `<div class="dashboard-night-week"><div class="dashboard-night-corner">시간대</div>${days.map((d,i)=>`<div class="dashboard-night-head"><b>${d}</b><small>${dates[i]?`${dates[i].getMonth()+1}/${dates[i].getDate()}`:""}</small></div>`).join("")}${cells.join("")}</div>`;
}

function normalizeProgramTitle(value) {
  return String(value || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^0-9a-z가-힣]/g, "");
}

function dashboardTodayProgramVisible(program){
  if(!program)return false;
  const title=String(program.actualTitle||program.title||"").trim();
  const kind=String(program.kind||"");
  const target=String(program.target||program.targetText||program.audience||program.participantScope||program.targetGrade||"").replace(/\s/g,"");
  // 대시보드 표시를 명시적으로 끈 프로그램은 어떤 출처에서도 다시 살리지 않는다.
  if(program.showTodayProgram===false||program.showOnDashboard===false||program.dashboardVisible===false||program.dashboardShow===false)return false;
  // 개학식·방학식 등 학교 전체 의식/행사는 '프로그램' 카드가 아니다.
  if(/개학식|방학식|입학식|졸업식/.test(title))return false;
  if(kind==="calendar-first-grade")return calendarEventLooksLikeProgram(program)&&!isWholeSchoolDashboardCalendarEvent(program);
  if(isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program))return false;
  // 공통활동 중 대상 텍스트가 전교생/전체학생/전학년이면 제외한다.
  if(kind==="common"&&/전교생|전체학생|전학년|학생전체|학교전체|1학년전체/.test(target))return false;
  // 공통활동의 참여명단이 현재 재학생 대부분을 덮는 경우도 전교생 자동참여로 본다.
  if(kind==="common"&&Array.isArray(program.students)&&program.students.length){
    const active=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).filter(studentIsCurrentlyActive);
    if(active.length>=20&&program.students.length>=Math.ceil(active.length*0.9))return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function todayProgramCollections(){
  const todayKey=dateKey(today);
  const connected=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).map(programWithOverride).filter(program=>program.date===todayKey&&!isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program)&&!(program.kind==="after"&&program.isCourseMaster));
  const connectedKeys=new Set(connected.map(program=>`${program.date}|${program.title}|${program.time}`));
  const local=(Array.isArray(state.programs)?state.programs:[]).filter(p=>p&&p.date===todayKey&&!connectedKeys.has(`${p.date}|${p.title}|${p.time}`)).map(p=>({...p,kind:"general",source:"이 PC 로컬"}));
  const afterGroups=afterSchoolProgramGroups().filter(p=>p.date===todayKey&&!p.isCourseMaster&&!connectedKeys.has(`${p.date}|${p.title}|${p.time}`));
  const all=[...connected,...local,...afterGroups];
  const knownTitles=all.map(item=>normalizeProgramTitle(item.actualTitle||item.title));
  // 학교캘린더에서 전교생 전체가 자동 참여하는 공통 일정은 오늘의 프로그램에 표시하지 않는다.
  // 09_공통활동마스터 자체를 제외하는 것이 아니라, 기존 학생 대상 공통·선택 프로그램은 그대로 유지한다.
  const calendarPrograms=schoolEventsForDate(todayKey).filter(event=>calendarEventTargetsFirstGrade(event)&&calendarEventLooksLikeProgram(event)&&!isWholeSchoolDashboardCalendarEvent(event)).filter(event=>{const title=normalizeProgramTitle(event.title);return !knownTitles.some(known=>known&&title&&(known.includes(title)||title.includes(known)));}).map(event=>({...event,kind:"calendar-first-grade"}));
  const selection=[...all.filter(p=>p.kind!=="after"),...calendarPrograms].filter(dashboardTodayProgramVisible).sort((a,b)=>String(a.time||"").localeCompare(String(b.time||"")));
  const after=all.filter(p=>p.kind==="after"||/방과후|야간심화|심화수업/.test(`${p.type||""} ${p.afterType||""} ${p.title||""}`)).filter(dashboardTodayProgramVisible).sort((a,b)=>String(a.time||"").localeCompare(String(b.time||"")));
  return {selection,after};
}
function dashboardProgramItem(p,group){
  if(p.kind==="calendar-first-grade")return `<button class="dashboard-program-row calendar-linked-program" data-school-event="${escapeHtml(p.id)}"><time>${escapeHtml(p.time||"종일")}</time><span><b>${escapeHtml(p.title)}</b><small>학교일정 연동 · ${escapeHtml(p.target||"1학년 대상")}${p.place?` · ${escapeHtml(p.place)}`:""}</small></span><em>일정 보기 ›</em></button>`;
  if(group==="after")return `<button class="dashboard-program-row program-area-after" data-after-program="${escapeHtml(p.id)}"><time>${escapeHtml(p.time||"시간 확인")}</time><span><b>${escapeHtml(p.actualTitle||p.title)}</b><small>${/야간심화/.test(`${p.afterType||""} ${p.title||""}`)?"야간심화":"방과후"} · ${escapeHtml(p.subject||"교과 미입력")} · ${escapeHtml(p.department||"부서 미입력")}</small></span><em>${Array.isArray(p.students)?p.students.length:0}명 ›</em></button>`;
  return `<button class="dashboard-program-row program-area-${programAreaKind(p)}" data-program="${escapeHtml(p.id)}"><time>${escapeHtml(p.time||"종일")}</time><span><b>${escapeHtml(p.actualTitle||p.title)}</b><small>${programAreaLabel(p)} · ${escapeHtml(p.place||"장소 확인")}</small></span><em>${Array.isArray(p.students)?p.students.length:0}명 ›</em></button>`;
}

let studentPopupClockTimer = null;
function updateStudentPopupClock() {
  const now = new Date();
  const date = $("#studentNowDate"), day = $("#studentNowDay"), clock = $("#studentNowClock"), period = $("#studentNowPeriod");
  if (!clock) return;
  date.textContent = `${now.getFullYear()}년 ${now.getMonth() + 1}월 ${now.getDate()}일`;
  day.textContent = `${["일", "월", "화", "수", "목", "금", "토"][now.getDay()]}요일`;
  clock.textContent = now.toLocaleTimeString("ko-KR", { hour12: true, hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", second: "2-digit" });
  period.textContent = schoolPeriodStatus(now).label;
}

function openStudentTimetableDrawer() {
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent = "STUDENT TIMETABLE";

  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  clearInterval(studentPopupClockTimer);
  updateStudentPopupClock();
  studentPopupClockTimer=setInterval(updateStudentPopupClock,1000);
}
function studentActivityOverview(student) {
  const matches=(item)=>String(item?.studentId||"")===String(student.id||"") || (student.studentNo && String(item?.studentNo||item?.number||"")===String(student.studentNo));
  const connected=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).map(programWithOverride).filter(program=>studentRecordWithinEnrollment(program,student)&&(program.students||[]).some(matches));
  const local=(state.programs||[]).filter(program=>studentRecordWithinEnrollment(program,student)&&(program.students||[]).some(matches));
  const programs=[...connected,...local].filter((program,index,array)=>array.findIndex(item=>`${item.id}|${item.date}|${item.title}`===`${program.id}|${program.date}|${program.title}`)===index).sort((a,b)=>String(b.date||"").localeCompare(String(a.date||"")));
  const activities=(readonlyCache?.studentActivities||[]).filter(item=>matches(item)&&studentRecordWithinEnrollment(item,student));
  const text=activities.map(item=>`${item.area} ${item.title} ${item.type} ${item.keywords}`).join(" ");
  const officer=/임원|회장|부회장|실장|부실장|반장|부반장/.test(text);
  const volunteer=/봉사/.test(text);
  const confirmedPrograms=programs.filter(program=>{const participant=(program.students||[]).find(matches);return participant && /출석|정상|참여|입실|명단QR|학사/.test(String(participant.result||"대상"));});
  return {programs,activities,officer,volunteer,confirmedPrograms,latest:programs.slice(0,3).map(program=>program.actualTitle||program.title).filter(Boolean)};
}

function printStudentDashboard(student){
  const source=document.querySelector("#studentDashboardPrint");
  if(!source)return toast("출력할 학생정보를 찾지 못했습니다.");
  const popup=window.open("","_blank","width=1200,height=900");
  if(!popup)return toast("인쇄 창을 열 수 없습니다.");
  const clone=source.cloneNode(true);
  const printHead=clone.querySelector(".student-dashboard-head > div");
  if(printHead)printHead.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend",studentPrintBadgesMarkup(student));
  const emptyCounsel=clone.querySelector('.student-counsel-history .empty'); if(emptyCounsel)emptyCounsel.closest('.student-counsel-history')?.remove();
  const analysisRows=studentVisibleAdmissionRows(student); const analysis=analysisRows.map(row=>{const group=row.group==='inseoul'?'인서울':'지거국',stage=row.stage==='core'?'핵심관리':row.stage==='growth'?'성장관리':'관심관리';const gaps=[];if(row.stage!=='core'){if(!row.internalOk&&row.base.actual9!=null)gaps.push(`내신 ${row.base.actual9.toFixed(2)}→${Number(row.standard.internal).toFixed(2)} 이내`);if(!row.mockCore)gaps.push(row.group==='inseoul'?'모의: 국·수·영 2개 2등급 이내 + 나머지 3등급 이내':'모의: 국·수·영 2개 4등급 이내 + 전체 5등급 이내');}return `<div><b>${group} ${stage}</b><span>${escapeHtml(row.reasons.join(' · '))}</span>${gaps.length?`<em>다음 단계 · ${escapeHtml(gaps.join(' / '))}</em>`:''}</div>`;}).join('');
  if(analysis){const score=clone.querySelector('.student-score-dashboard');score?.insertAdjacentHTML('afterend',`<section class="student-print-analysis"><h4>학생 성장·진학 분석</h4>${analysis}</section>`);}
  clone.querySelectorAll(".no-print,button").forEach(el=>el.remove());
  popup.document.write(`<!doctype html><html lang="ko"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo)}_${escapeHtml(student.name)}_학생개인대시보드</title><style>
  @page{size:A4 landscape;margin:8mm}*{box-sizing:border-box}html,body{margin:0;background:#fff;color:#233b4d;font-family:"Malgun Gothic",sans-serif}body{padding:0}.student-dashboard-v424{width:100%;max-width:none;padding:0}.student-dashboard-head{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;border-bottom:2px solid #65b7a8;padding-bottom:4mm;margin-bottom:4mm}.student-dashboard-head h2{margin:1mm 0;font-size:18pt}.student-dashboard-head small{font-size:8pt}.student-dashboard-grid{display:grid;gap:3mm}.student-dashboard-grid.top{grid-template-columns:1.05fr 1.05fr 1.25fr}.student-dashboard-grid.detail{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}.student-dashboard-grid section,.student-score-dashboard{border:1px solid #cbdde5;border-radius:3mm;padding:3.5mm;break-inside:avoid;page-break-inside:avoid;background:#fbfdfd}.student-dashboard-grid.top section:nth-child(1){background:#f4fbf8}.student-dashboard-grid.top section:nth-child(2){background:#f7f9fd}.student-dashboard-grid.top section:nth-child(3){background:#fffaf1}.student-dashboard-grid h4,.student-score-dashboard h4{margin:0 0 2mm;color:#15947f;font-size:11pt}dl{display:grid;grid-template-columns:26mm 1fr;gap:1mm 2mm;margin:0;font-size:8.5pt}dt{font-weight:700;color:#557082}dd{margin:0;overflow-wrap:anywhere}.attendance-kpis{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:2mm}.attendance-kpis span{background:#f0f6f8;border-radius:2mm;text-align:center;padding:2mm;font-size:8pt}.attendance-kpis b{display:block;font-size:14
--- match 10 at 1080622 ---
 data-font-minus>−</button><output id="widgetFontScale">${setting.fontScale || 100}%</output><button type="button" data-font-plus>＋</button></div></label></div>${key === "timetable" ? `<div class="alert-settings"><label><input type="checkbox" id="showPeriodTimes" ${setting.showTimes !== false ? "checked" : ""}> 교시별 시간 표시</label><label><input type="checkbox" id="startPeriodAlert" ${setting.startAlert ? "checked" : ""}> 수업 시작 알림</label><label><input type="checkbox" id="endPeriodAlert" ${setting.endAlert ? "checked" : ""}> 수업 종료 알림</label></div>` : ""}<button class="btn primary">로컬 설정 저장</button><p class="drawer-note">위젯 설정은 이 PC에만 저장됩니다. 데이터 원본은 수정하지 않습니다.</p></form>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  let scale = setting.fontScale || 100;
  const updateScale = () => ($("#widgetFontScale").textContent = `${scale}%`);
  $("[data-font-minus]").onclick = () => {
    scale = Math.max(80, scale - 10);
    updateScale();
  };
  $("[data-font-plus]").onclick = () => {
    scale = Math.min(130, scale + 10);
    updateScale();
  };
  $("#widgetSettingsForm").onsubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    state.settings.widgetSettings[key] = {
      ...setting,
      visible: $("#widgetVisible").value === "true",
      width: $("#widgetWidth").value,
      fontScale: scale,
      ...(key === "timetable"
        ? {
            showTimes: $("#showPeriodTimes").checked,
            startAlert: $("#startPeriodAlert").checked,
            endAlert: $("#endPeriodAlert").checked,
          }
        : {}),
    };
    if (key === "timetable" && (state.settings.widgetSettings[key].startAlert || state.settings.widgetSettings[key].endAlert)) {
      try { if ("Notification" in window && Notification.permission === "default") await Notification.requestPermission(); } catch {}
    }
    await save();
    closeDrawer();
    render(state.activePage);
    toast("위젯 설정을 저장했습니다.");
  };
}


function bindProgramRosterGradeFilter() {
  const rows = [...document.querySelectorAll('[data-program-student-grade]')];
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-program-grade]').forEach(button=>{
    button.onclick=()=>{
      const grade=button.dataset.programGrade||'all';
      document.querySelectorAll('[data-program-grade]').forEach(item=>item.classList.toggle('active',item===button));
      rows.forEach(row=>row.classList.toggle('hidden',grade!=='all'&&row.dataset.programStudentGrade!==grade));
      const visible=rows.filter(row=>!row.classList.contains('hidden')).length;
      const count=document.querySelector('#programRosterVisibleCount'); if(count) count.textContent=`${visible}명 표시`;
    };
  });
}
function programRosterTabs(students) {
  const counts={1:0,2:0,3:0}; students.forEach(student=>{const grade=programStudentGrade(student);if(counts[grade]!=null)counts[grade]+=1;});
  return `<div class="program-roster-filter"><div><button class="active" data-program-grade="all">전체 ${students.length}명</button>${[1,2,3].map(grade=>`<button data-program-grade="${grade}" ${counts[grade]?"":"disabled"}>${grade}학년 ${counts[grade]}명</button>`).join("")}</div><span id="programRosterVisibleCount">${students.length}명 표시</span></div>`;
}
function openNightProgramSummary(id, studentKey="", day="", slot=""){
  const all=[...afterSchoolProgramGroups(),...groupedAfterSchoolCourses(afterSchoolProgramGroups()),...(readonlyCache?.programs||[])];
  const source=all.find(item=>String(item.id)===String(id));
  const program=programWithOverride(source);
  if(!program) return openAfterschoolProgramDrawer(id);
  const display=nightProgramStatus(program);
  const student=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(x=>attendanceStudentKey(x)===String(studentKey)||String(x.id)===String(studentKey)||String(x.studentNo)===String(studentKey));
  const participant=(program.students||[]).find(x=>String(x.studentId||"")===String(student?.id||"")||String(x.studentNo||x.number||"")===String(student?.studentNo||""));
  $("#drawerKicker").textContent="NIGHT PROGRAM SUMMARY";
  $("#drawerTitle").textContent=program.actualTitle||program.title||display.label;
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML=`<div class="night-program-summary-card"><div class="program-name-pair"><div><small>표시 구분</small><b>${escapeHtml(display.label)}</b></div><div><small>프로그램명</small><b>${escapeHtml(program.actualTitle||program.title||"-")}</b></div></div><div class="program-summary afterschool-program-summary"><div><b>학생</b><span>${escapeHtml(student?`${student.studentNo} ${student.name}`:"-")}</span></div><div><b>해당 차시</b><span>${escapeHtml(day||program.date||"-")} ${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(day||program.date,true))}</span></div><div><b>시간대</b><span>${escapeHtml(slot||"-")}</span></div><div><b>운영시간</b><span>${escapeHtml(program.time||"-")}</span></div><div><b>운영요일</b><span>${escapeHtml(program.weekdays||weekdayLabelFromKey(program.date,true)||"-")}</span></div><div><b>장소</b><span>${escapeHtml(program.place||"-")}</span></div><div><b>담당교사</b><span>${escapeHtml(program.teacher||"-")}</span></div><div><b>유형</b><span>${escapeHtml(program.afterType||program.type||"프로그램")}</span></div><div><b>참여상태</b><span>${escapeHtml(participant?.attendance||participant?.participation||participant?.result||"참여")}</span></div><div><b>확인방식</b><span>${escapeHtml(participant?.checkMethod||"출석부 연결")}</span></div></div><div class="program-notice attendance-linked"><b>표시 기준</b><p>11_방과후학교의 강좌, 12_차시일정의 실제수업일(있으면) 또는 운영일자·예정일, 13_출석부의 학생 등록을 연결해 표시합니다.</p></div><div class="program-actions"><button class="btn primary" id="openNightProgramFull">전체 프로그램 상세</button></div></div>`;
  $("#drawer").classList.remove("program-expanded","hidden"); $("#drawerBackdrop").classList.remove("hidden");
  $("#openNightProgramFull")?.addEventListener("click",()=>openAfterschoolProgramDrawer(id));
}

function normalizeAfterAttendanceStatus(value){
  const text=String(value||"").trim();
  if(/불참\(인정\)|공결|공식 ?행사|인정/.test(text))return "불참(인정)";
  if(/불참\(조퇴\)|조퇴/.test(text))return "불참(조퇴)";
  if(/불참\(결석\)|병결|결석/.test(text))return "불참(결석)";
  if(/불참\(미인정\)|미인정|무단|불참/.test(text))return "불참(미인정)";
  if(/수강|출석|참여|정상|입실|지각/.test(text))return "수강";
  return "신청";
}
function afterAttendanceOverrideKey(program,student){
  return [program.sessionId||program.id||"",student.studentId||student.studentNo||student.number||student.name||""].join("|");
}
function resolvedAfterAttendanceStatus(program,student){
  const key=afterAttendanceOverrideKey(program,student);
  return state.afterAttendanceOverrides?.[key]||normalizeAfterAttendanceStatus(student.attendance||student.result||student.participation);
}

      window.open(`mailto:?bcc=${encodeURIComponent(recipients.join(','))}&subject=${encodeURIComponent(mailSubject)}&body=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`,'_self');
      toast(`${recipients.length}명의 메일 발송 화면을 열었습니다.`);
      return;
    }
    try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);toast(`${title} 내용을 복사했습니다.`);}catch{toast('클립보드 복사에 실패했습니다.');}
  };
}
function xmlEscape(value){return String(value??"").replace(/&/g,"&amp;").replace(/</g,"&lt;").replace(/>/g,"&gt;").replace(/\"/g,"&quot;");}
function safeFileName(value){return String(value||"출석부").replace(/[\\/:*?\"<>|]/g,"_").slice(0,80);}
function exportProgramAttendanceExcel(program){
  const rows=(program.students||[]).map((student,index)=>[index+1,student.studentNo||student.number||"",student.name||"",student.className?`${student.className}반`:"","",""]);
  const meta=[["프로그램명",program.actualTitle||program.title||""],["운영일",program.date||""],["운영시간",program.time||""],["장소",program.place||""],["교과",program.subject||""],["담당부서",program.department||""],["담당교사",program.teacher||""]];
  const rowXml=(cells)=>`<Row>${cells.map(cell=>`<Cell><Data ss:Type=\"String\">${xmlEscape(cell)}</Data></Cell>`).join("")}</Row>`;
  const xml=`<?xml version=\"1.0\"?><Workbook xmlns=\"urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:spreadsheet\" xmlns:ss=\"urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:spreadsheet\"><Worksheet ss:Name=\"출석부\"><Table>${meta.map(rowXml).join("")}<Row></Row>${rowXml(["번호","학번","이름","반","출석","비고"])}${rows.map(rowXml).join("")}</Table></Worksheet></Workbook>`;
  const blob=new Blob(["\ufeff",xml],{type:"application/vnd.ms-excel"}); const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob); const a=document.createElement("a"); a.href=url; a.download=`${safeFileName(program.actualTitle||program.title)}_출석부.xls`; document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove(); setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000); toast("출석부 Excel 파일을 생성했습니다.");
}

function dormProgramTermKey(program){
  const explicit=String(program.semester||program.term||'');
  if(/2학년\s*1|2-1/.test(explicit))return 'g2s1';
  if(/2학년\s*2|2-2/.test(explicit))return 'g2s2';
  if(/3학년/.test(explicit))return 'g3';
  const date=String(program.date||program.startDate||'');
  const year=date.slice(0,4),month=Number(date.slice(5,7));
  if(year==='2026')return month&&month<=7?'g1s1':'g1s2';
  if(year==='2027')return month&&month<=7?'g2s1':'g2s2';
  if(year==='2028')return 'g3';
  return '';
}
function dormProgramIsHolisticTarget(program){
  return program?.kind==='selected'||(program?.kind==='common'&&program?.reportRequired===true);
}
function dormTermActivityStats(student,termMode){
  const view=studentActivityView(student);
  const order=['g1s1','g1s2','g2s1','g2s2','g3'];
  const cutoff=Math.max(0,order.indexOf(termMode));
  const allowed=new Set(order.slice(0,cutoff+1));
  const programs=(view.holisticPrograms||[]).filter(p=>allowed.has(dormProgramTermKey(p)));
  const canonical=studentCanonicalReports(student);
  const reportTargets=programs.filter(p=>programRequiresStudentReport(p,student,canonical));
  const reports=[...new Map(reportTargets.map(p=>resolveStudentProgramReport(p,student,canonical)).filter(Boolean).map(r=>[canonicalReportKey(r),r])).values()];
  return {programs,reportTargets,reports,submitted:reports.length,reportRate:reportTargets.length?Math.min(100,reports.length/reportTargets.length*100):0};
}
function dormCohortAverage(termMode){
  const roster=dormTermRoster(termMode);
  const stats=roster.map(row=>{
    const student=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(s=>String(s.id)===String(row.studentId)||String(s.studentNo)===String(row.studentNo));
    return student?dormTermActivityStats(student,termMode):null;
  }).filter(Boolean);
  const avg=(key)=>stats.length?stats.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+Number(x[key]||0),0)/stats.length:0;
  return {count:stats.length,programs:stats.length?stats.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+x.programs.length,0)/stats.length:0,reportTargets:stats.length?stats.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+(x.reportTargets?.length||0),0)/stats.length:0,submitted:avg('submitted'),reportRate:avg('reportRate')};
}
function dormProgramActivityMarkup(student,termMode,bundle){
  const termStats=dormTermActivityStats(student,termMode),cohort=dormCohortAverage(termMode);
  const unified=studentActivityView(student);

  const after=allAfter.filter(p=>!night.includes(p));
  const uniqueTitles=rows=>[...new Set(rows.map(p=>String(p.actualTitle||p.title||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))];
  const attendance=(readonlyCache?.nightAttendance||[]).filter(r=>studentMatches(r,student));
  const programRows=termStats.programs.map(p=>{
    const report=p.report||resolveStudentProgramReport(p,student,termStats.reports);
    const submitted=Boolean(report&&report.submitted!==false);return `<li class="${submitted?'has-report':''}"><b>${escapeHtml(p.recordTitle||p.actualTitle||p.title||'프로그램')}</b>${submitted?`<button type="button" class="state positive dorm-report-open" data-dorm-report-id="${escapeHtml(report.id||'')}" data-dorm-report-student="${escapeHtml(student.id||'')}">보고서 제출 · 보기</button>`:`<span class="state warn">보고서 미제출</span>`}</li>`;
  }).join('');
  return `<div class="dorm-activity-grid"><article><h4>${escapeHtml(dormTermDefinition(termMode)?.label||'현재')} 프로그램·보고서</h4><div class="dorm-cohort-compare"><span><small>본인 프로그램</small><b>${termStats.programs.length}</b></span><span><small>학사생 평균</small><b>${cohort.programs.toFixed(1)}</b></span><span><small>본인 보고서율</small><b>${Math.round(termStats.reportRate)}%</b><em>${termStats.submitted}/${termStats.reportTargets?.length||0}</em></span><span><small>학사생 평균</small><b>${Math.round(cohort.reportRate)}%</b><em>대상 ${cohort.reportTargets.toFixed(1)}</em></span></div><ul>${programRows||'<li class="empty-inline">해당 학기 프로그램 자료 없음</li>'}</ul></article><article><h4>방과후·야간심화 근황</h4><p><b>방과후</b> ${escapeHtml(uniqueTitles(after).join(' · ')||'참여 기록 없음')}</p><p><b>야간심화</b> ${escapeHtml(uniqueTitles(night).join(' · ')||'참여 기록 없음')}</p><p><b>야자 출석기록</b> ${attendance.length}건</p></article></div>`;

function dormInseoulGap(student){
  const row=admissionManagementDataset('inseoul').find(x=>String(x.student?.id||'')===String(student.id||''));
  const standard=row?.standard||{internal:uepManagementRule('inseoul').internal,mockText:uepManagementRule('inseoul').mockText};
  const actual=row?.base?.actual9;
  const internalGap=actual==null?null:Math.max(0,(actual-Number(standard.internal))/Number(standard.internal)*100);
  const levels=(row?.mock?.mainLevels||[]).filter(Number.isFinite);
  const within3=levels.filter(v=>v<=3).length,within2=levels.filter(v=>v<=2).length;
  const mockScore=levels.length?Math.min(100,((Math.min(within3,3)+Math.min(within2,2))/5)*100):null;
  const mockGap=mockScore==null?null:100-mockScore;
  const overall=[internalGap,mockGap].filter(v=>v!=null).length?Math.max(...[internalGap,mockGap].filter(v=>v!=null)):null;
  const gaps=[];
  if(actual==null)gaps.push('내신 9등급 환산자료 확인 필요');
  else if(actual>standard.internal)gaps.push(`내신 ${actual.toFixed(2)} → 핵심기준 ${Number(standard.internal).toFixed(2)} 이내 보완`);
  else gaps.push(`내신 핵심기준 충족 (${actual.toFixed(2)})`);
  if(levels.length<3)gaps.push('국·수·영 모의고사 자료 보완');
  else{
    levels.forEach((v,i)=>{if(v>3)gaps.push(`${['국어','수학','영어'][i]||'주요영역'} ${v}등급 → 3등급 이내`);});
    if(within2<2)gaps.push(`국·수·영 2등급 이내 ${within2}개 → 2개 필요`);
    if(within3===3&&within2>=2)gaps.push('모의고사 인서울 핵심기준 충족');
  }
  return {row,standard,actual,internalGap,mockGap,overall,gaps};
}

function dormProgramIsHolisticTarget(program){
  return program?.kind==='selected'||(program?.kind==='common'&&program?.reportRequired===true);
}
function dormTermActivityStats(student,termMode){
  const view=studentActivityView(student);
  const order=['g1s1','g1s2','g2s1','g2s2','g3'];
  const cutoff=Math.max(0,order.indexOf(termMode));
  const allowed=new Set(order.slice(0,cutoff+1));
  const programs=(view.holisticPrograms||[]).filter(p=>allowed.has(dormProgramTermKey(p)));
  const canonical=studentCanonicalReports(student);
  const reportTargets=programs.filter(p=>programRequiresStudentReport(p,student,canonical));
  const reports=[...new Map(reportTargets.map(p=>resolveStudentProgramReport(p,student,canonical)).filter(Boolean).map(r=>[canonicalReportKey(r),r])).values()];
  return {programs,reportTargets,reports,submitted:reports.length,reportRate:reportTargets.length?Math.min(100,reports.length/reportTargets.length*100):0};
}
function dormCohortAverage(termMode){
  const roster=dormTermRoster(termMode);
  const stats=roster.map(row=>{
    const student=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(s=>String(s.id)===String(row.studentId)||String(s.studentNo)===String(row.studentNo));
    return student?dormTermActivityStats(student,termMode):null;
  }).filter(Boolean);
  const avg=(key)=>stats.length?stats.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+Number(x[key]||0),0)/stats.length:0;
  return {count:stats.length,programs:stats.length?stats.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+x.programs.length,0)/stats.length:0,reportTargets:stats.length?stats.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+(x.reportTargets?.length||0),0)/stats.length:0,submitted:avg('submitted'),reportRate:avg('reportRate')};
}

  const after=allAfter.filter(p=>!night.includes(p));
  const uniqueTitles=rows=>[...new Set(rows.map(p=>String(p.actualTitle||p.title||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))];
  const attendance=(readonlyCache?.nightAttendance||[]).filter(r=>studentMatches(r,student));
  const programRows=termStats.programs.map(p=>{

    const submitted=Boolean(report&&report.submitted!==false);return `<li class="${submitted?'has-report':''}"><b>${escapeHtml(p.recordTitle||p.actualTitle||p.title||'프로그램')}</b>${submitted?`<button type="button" class="state positive dorm-report-open" data-dorm-report-id="${escapeHtml(report.id||'')}" data-dorm-report-student="${escapeHtml(student.id||'')}">보고서 제출 · 보기</button>`:`<span class="state warn">보고서 미제출</span>`}</li>`;
  }).join('');

}

function dormComprehensiveCounselMarkup(student,termMode,bundle){
  const gap=dormInseoulGap(student),career=studentCareerSupport(student,bundle),stats=dormTermActivityStats(student,termMode),cohort=dormCohortAverage(termMode);
  const selected=(bundle.selected||[]).filter(r=>String(r.final||'Y')!=='N');
  const points=[...gap.gaps];
  if(!selected.length)points.push('희망계열에 맞는 선택과목 이수계획 확인 필요');
  if(stats.programs.length<cohort.programs)points.push(`계열 연계 프로그램: 본인 ${stats.programs.length}개 / 학사생 평균 ${cohort.programs.toFixed(1)}개`);
  if(stats.reportRate+1<cohort.reportRate)points.push(`보고서 제출률: 본인 ${Math.round(stats.reportRate)}% / 학사생 평균 ${Math.round(cohort.reportRate)}%`);
  points.push(`진로희망 ${career.hopeTrack} · ${career.hopeMajor}와 선택과목·탐구활동의 연속성 확인`);
  return `<section class="dorm-counsel-priority"><header><div><small>SEOUL · CORE GAP</small><h4>인서울 핵심관리 괴리 최우선</h4></div><strong>${gap.overall==null?'자료 확인':gap.overall<=0?'핵심기준 충족':`최대 괴리 ${gap.overall.toFixed(0)}%`}</strong></header><div class="dorm-gap-metrics"><span><small>내신 핵심기준</small><b>9등급 ${Number(gap.standard.internal).toFixed(2)} 이내</b></span><span><small>현재 내신</small><b>${gap.actual==null?'-':gap.actual.toFixed(2)}</b><em>${gap.internalGap==null?'자료 없음':gap.internalGap===0?'충족':`괴리 +${gap.internalGap.toFixed(0)}%`}</em></span><span><small>모의 핵심기준</small><b>국·수·영 2개≤2, 전체≤3</b></span><span><small>모의 충족 괴리</small><b>${gap.mockGap==null?'-':`${gap.mockGap.toFixed(0)}%`}</b></span></div><ol>${points.map(p=>`<li>${escapeHtml(p)}</li>`).join('')}</ol><p class="dorm-holistic-note"><b>학생부종합전형 관점</b> 성적만으로 순위를 매기지 않고 진로희망–선택과목–계열연계 프로그램–보고서 탐구의 연결성과 지속성을 함께 상담합니다.</p></section>`;
}

  if(dormMode==='outing'){

  const cards=roster.map(row=>dormGrowthCard(row,term)).join('');

}

function attendanceStudentKey(row) {
  // 출결 원본은 학생ID가 비어 있거나 서로 다른 형식일 수 있으므로 학번을 최우선 기준으로 사용합니다.
  return String(row?.studentNo || row?.studentId || `${row?.name || ""}|${row?.className || ""}`);
}
function nightRecordIsAttendance(row) {
  const text = `${row?.result || ""} ${row?.detail || ""} ${row?.type || ""}`;
  if (/결석|미입실|미제출|불참/.test(text)) return false;
  return /출석|정상|명단QR|QR|입실/.test(text) || Boolean(row?.time || row?.period);
}

function singlePeriodLabel(session) {
  const candidates=[session?.period,session?.startTime,session?.time].map(v=>String(v||"").trim()).filter(Boolean);
  for(const text of candidates){
    const m=text.match(/(\d+)교시/);
    if(m) return `${m[1]}교시`;
  }
  return String(session?.time||session?.period||"시간 확인");
}
function programOverlapsSlot(program, slot) {
  if(isWednesdayKey(program?.date)||!programHasNightStudyImpact(program)) return false;
  const descriptor = `${program?.time || ""} ${program?.title || ""} ${program?.actualTitle || ""} ${program?.type || ""}`;
  // 차시 시작시각이 8교시라면 시트의 기존 야자영향타임 값보다 학교 운영 기준을 우선한다.
  // 즉 8교시는 야자1이 아니라 오후자습으로만 표시한다.
  if (/8교시/.test(descriptor)) return slot === "오후자습";
  const explicitSlots=(program?.nightSlots||[]).flatMap(value=>{
    const text=String(value||"");
    return ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].filter(name=>text.includes(name));
  });
  if(explicitSlots.length) return explicitSlots.includes(slot);
  const range = slotRange(slot);
  const namedSlot = nightSlotName(descriptor);
  if (namedSlot && ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].includes(namedSlot) && !String(namedSlot).includes("기타")) return namedSlot === slot;
  const clocks = descriptor.match(/\d{1,2}:\d{2}/g) || [];
  if (!range || clocks.length < 2) return Boolean(program?.affectsAttendance && /오후자습/.test(descriptor) && slot === "오후자습");
  const start = minutesOfClock(clocks[0]), end = minutesOfClock(clocks[1]);
  return Number.isFinite(start) && Number.isFinite(end) && start < range[1] && end > range[0];
}
function programParticipationFor(student, day, slot) {
  const id = String(student?.id || student?.studentId || "");
  const number = String(student?.studentNo || "");
  const connected = readonlyCache?.programs || [];
  const derived = typeof afterSchoolProgramGroups === "function" ? afterSchoolProgramGroups() : [];
  const seen = new Set();
  const programs = [...connected, ...derived].filter(program => {
    const key = `${program.id || ""}|${program.date || ""}|${program.title || ""}|${program.time || ""}`;
    if (seen.has(key)) return false; seen.add(key); return true;
  });
  for (const program of programs) {
    if(isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program))continue;
    if (program.date !== day || !programOverlapsSlot(program, slot)) continue;
    const target = (program.students || []).find(item => String(item.studentId || "") === id || (number && String(item.studentNo || item.number || "") === number));
    if (!target) continue;
    const participationText = [target.result,target.attendance,target.participation,target.status,target.checkMethod].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
    const inactive = /결석|불참|취소|중도포기|퇴강|미참여/.test(participationText);
    // 13_출석부의 활성 수강명단은 출석상태가 '등록'이어도 해당 실제 차시에는 프로그램 참여자로 본다.
    // 실제 수업일·야자영향타임은 program.date/program.time 단계에서 이미 검증되므로 명단 등록만으로 다른 날짜에 표시되지 않는다.
    const confirmed = !inactive && ((typeof nightRecordIsAttendance === "function" && nightRecordIsAttendance(target)) || /출석|정상|참여|입실|명단QR|학사|등록|신청/.test(participationText));
    return {program, target, confirmed, pending: !confirmed && !inactive};
  }
  return null;
}
function nightProgramStatus(program){
  const text=`${program?.afterType||""} ${program?.type||""} ${program?.actualTitle||program?.title||""}`;
  if(/야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)) return {cls:"after",label:"야간심화",kind:"after"};
  if(/일반 방과후|방학 방과후|방과후학교/.test(text)) return {cls:"after",label:"방과후",kind:"after"};
  const title=String(program?.actualTitle||program?.title||"프로그램");
  return {cls:"program",label:title.length>8?`${title.slice(0,7)}…`:title,kind:program?.kind||"general"};
}
function attendanceIsWeekday(day){const n=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`).getDay();return n>=1&&n<=5;}
function attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(day){
  const key=String(day||'').slice(0,10);
  const fixed2026=new Set(['2026-01-01','2026-02-16','2026-02-17','2026-02-18','2026-03-01','2026-03-02','2026-05-05','2026-05-24','2026-05-25','2026-06-06','2026-08-15','2026-08-17','2026-09-24','2026-09-25','2026-09-26','2026-10-03','2026-10-05','2026-10-09','2026-12-25']);
  if(fixed2026.has(key))return true;
  const closureRx=/공휴일|대체공휴일|재량휴업|휴업일|개교기념일|임시공휴일|석가탄신일|부처님오신날|어린이날|현충일|광복절|개천절|한글날|성탄절|설날|추석/;
  return schoolEventsForDate(key).some(event=>{
    const title=String(event.title||'').trim(); const type=String(event.type||'').trim();
    if(!closureRx.test(title+' '+type))return false;
    if(/공휴일|대체공휴일|재량휴업|휴업일|개교기념일|임시공휴일/.test(title+' '+type))return true;
    const start=String(event.date||'').slice(0,10), end=String(event.endDate||event.date||'').slice(0,10);
    return start===key&&end===key;
  });
}
function isNightOperatingDay(day){return attendanceIsWeekday(day)&&!attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(day);}
function nearestNightOperatingDay(day){
  let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
  for(let i=0;i<31;i++){const key=dateKey(d);if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;d.setDate(d.getDate()-1);}
  return day;
}
function shiftNightOperatingDate(day,direction){
  let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
  const step=Number(direction)>=0?1:-1;
  for(let i=0;i<62;i++){
    d.setDate(d.getDate()+step);
    const key=dateKey(d);
    if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;
  }
  return day;
}

}
function nightProgramStatus(program){
  const text=`${program?.afterType||""} ${program?.type||""} ${program?.actualTitle||program?.title||""}`;
  if(/야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)) return {cls:"after",label:"야간심화",kind:"after"};
  if(/일반 방과후|방학 방과후|방과후학교/.test(text)) return {cls:"after",label:"방과후",kind:"after"};
  const title=String(program?.actualTitle||program?.title||"프로그램");
  return {cls:"program",label:title.length>8?`${title.slice(0,7)}…`:title,kind:program?.kind||"general"};
}
function attendanceIsWeekday(day){const n=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`).getDay();return n>=1&&n<=5;}
function attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(day){
  const key=String(day||'').slice(0,10);
  const fixed2026=new Set(['2026-01-01','2026-02-16','2026-02-17','2026-02-18','2026-03-01','2026-03-02','2026-05-05','2026-05-24','2026-05-25','2026-06-06','2026-08-15','2026-08-17','2026-09-24','2026-09-25','2026-09-26','2026-10-03','2026-10-05','2026-10-09','2026-12-25']);
  if(fixed2026.has(key))return true;
  const closureRx=/공휴일|대체공휴일|재량휴업|휴업일|개교기념일|임시공휴일|석가탄신일|부처님오신날|어린이날|현충일|광복절|개천절|한글날|성탄절|설날|추석/;
  return schoolEventsForDate(key).some(event=>{
    const title=String(event.title||'').trim(); const type=String(event.type||'').trim();
    if(!closureRx.test(title+' '+type))return false;
    if(/공휴일|대체공휴일|재량휴업|휴업일|개교기념일|임시공휴일/.test(title+' '+type))return true;
    const start=String(event.date||'').slice(0,10), end=String(event.endDate||event.date||'').slice(0,10);
    return start===key&&end===key;
  });
}
function isNightOperatingDay(day){return attendanceIsWeekday(day)&&!attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(day);}
function nearestNightOperatingDay(day){
  let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
  for(let i=0;i<31;i++){const key=dateKey(d);if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;d.setDate(d.getDate()-1);}
  return day;
}
function shiftNightOperatingDate(day,direction){
  let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
  const step=Number(direction)>=0?1:-1;
  for(let i=0;i<62;i++){
    d.setDate(d.getDate()+step);
    const key=dateKey(d);
    if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;
  }
  return day;
}

async function deleteLateAttendanceRow(row){
  if(currentRoleId()!=="admin"||!row?.id)return;
  if(!confirm(`${row.date} ${row.studentNo} ${row.name} 지각 기록을 삭제할까요?`))return;
  try{const result=await window.schoolBoard.deleteLateAttendance(row.id);if(result?.patch)readonlyCache={...(readonlyCache||{}),...result.patch};render("attendance");toast("지각 기록을 삭제했습니다.");}catch(error){toast(error?.message||"지각 삭제에 실패했습니다.");}
}

function attendancePersonalStatisticsView(officialRows,lateRows,nightRows) {
  const students=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo||"").localeCompare(String(b.studentNo||"")));
  if(attendanceStatStudentFilter!=="all"&&!students.some(s=>attendanceStudentKey(s)===attendanceStatStudentFilter))attendanceStatStudentFilter="all";
  const selectedStudent=attendanceStatStudentFilter==="all"?null:students.find(s=>attendanceStudentKey(s)===attendanceStatStudentFilter);
  const filterRows=rows=>selectedStudent?rows.filter(row=>attendanceStudentKey(row)===attendanceStudentKey(selectedStudent)):rows;
  const official=filterRows(officialRows).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)));
  const late=filterRows(lateRows).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)));
  const night=filterRows(nightRows).filter(row=>{
    const sourceText=`${row.result||""} ${row.type||""} ${row.affiliation||""} ${row.detail||""}`;
    const dormOnly=/학사/.test(sourceText)&&!/방과후|야간심화|공동교육과정|프로그램|출석|지각/.test(sourceText);
    return !dormOnly;
  }).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)));

  const history=(label,rows,value)=>`<section class="attendance-personal-history"><header><h3>${label}</h3><span>${rows.length}건 · ${dates(rows).length}일</span></header>${rows.length?rows.slice(0,60).map(row=>`<article><time>${escapeHtml(row.date||"-")}</time><b>${escapeHtml(value(row))}</b><p>${escapeHtml(row.reason||row.detail||row.result||row.programTitle||"")}</p></article>`).join(""):'<div class="empty-inline">기록이 없습니다.</div>'}</section>`;

}

}

  const out=[];
  for(let i=0;i<files.length;i+=per){
    const pair=files.slice(i,i+per); const first=pair[0]||{};
    const text=pair.map(x=>String(x.text||'')).filter(Boolean).join('\n\n');
    const draft=inputCenterDraftFromPayload(first,text);
    draft.bundleId=`BUNDLE-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8)}`;
    draft.pages=pair.map((x,j)=>({page:j+1,filePath:x.path||'',fileName:x.name||'',extension:x.extension||'',size:Number(x.size||0),rotation:0}));
    draft.pageCount=pair.length; draft.fileName=pair.map(x=>x.name).filter(Boolean).join(' + ')||(first.name||'스캔 원본');
    draft.matchStatus=draft.studentNo?draft.matchStatus:'판독 대기';
    draft.bundleStatus=pair.length===per?`${per}페이지 묶음 · 순서/회전 확인`:`예상 ${per}페이지 중 ${pair.length}페이지 · 확인 필요`;
    draft.batchScope=inputCenterConfig.batchScope; draft.batchGrade=inputCenterConfig.batchGrade; draft.batchClass=inputCenterConfig.batchClass;
    out.push(draft);
  }
  return out;
}
function inputCenterIsCompleted(row={}){return /정규화완료|적용완료/.test(String(row.status||row.processingStatus||''));}
async function refreshInputCenterSourceSilently(){
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.refreshInputCenterSource)return false;
  try{const result=await window.schoolBoard.refreshInputCenterSource();if(!result?.ok)return false;readonlyCache={...(readonlyCache||{}),externalInputs:result.externalInputs||[],inputCenterSyncedAt:result.inputCenterSyncedAt||new Date().toISOString()};return true;}catch{return false;}
}
function inputCenterAllHistory(){return (readonlyCache?.externalInputs||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>String(b.createdAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.createdAt||'')));}
function inputCenterHistory(){const rows=inputCenterAllHistory();return inputCenterConfig.historyMode==='completed'?rows.filter(inputCenterIsCompleted):rows.filter(x=>!inputCenterIsCompleted(x));}
function inputCenterLibraryRows(){
  const remote=inputCenterHistory().map(x=>({...x,storageMode:x.storageMode||'uep'}));
  // 02 검수·분류와 03 UEP 적용은 [UEP]입력센터 원본을 단일 기준으로 사용합니다.
  // 예전 PC 로컬 보관 이력은 새 정규화 파이프라인에 섞지 않습니다.
  if(inputCenterConfig.libraryScope!=='local') return remote.sort((a,b)=>String(b.createdAt||b.savedAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.createdAt||a.savedAt||'')));
  return (inputCenterLocalHistory||[]).map(x=>({...x,storageMode:'local'})).sort((a,b)=>String(b.createdAt||b.savedAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.createdAt||a.savedAt||'')));
}
function inputCenterProgramSemester(p={}){
  const text=`${p.semester||''} ${p.term||''} ${p.schoolTerm||''} ${p.date||''} ${p.startDate||''}`;
  if(/2학기|(^|[^0-9])2([^0-9]|$)/.test(text)) return '2';
  if(/1학기|(^|[^0-9])1([^0-9]|$)/.test(text)) return '1';
  const m=text.match(/2026[-/.](\d{1,2})/); if(m) return Number(m[1])<=7?'1':'2';
  return '';
}
function inputCenterProgramGroup(p={}){
  const text=`${p.kind||''} ${p.type||''} ${p.afterType||''} ${p.actualTitle||''} ${p.title||''}`;
  if(p.kind==='after'||/방과후|야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)) return /야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)?'night':'after';
  if(p.kind==='common'||/공통/.test(text)) return 'common';
  if(p.kind==='selected'||/선택/.test(text)) return 'selected';
  return '';
}
function inputCenterProgramIdentity(p={}){
  const group=inputCenterProgramGroup(p);
  const title=String(p.actualTitle||p.title||p.name||'').normalize('NFKC').trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ');
  const teacher=String(p.teacher||p.teacherName||p.instructor||'').normalize('NFKC').trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ');
  const semester=inputCenterProgramSemester(p);
  // 방과후/야간심화 readonly 데이터는 차시·출석 결합 과정에서 행별 임시 ID가 달라질 수 있습니다.
  // 연결목록은 강좌 마스터를 선택하는 곳이므로 학기+유형+강좌명+담당교사 기준으로 1회만 노출합니다.
  if(group==='after'||group==='night') return `${group}|${semester}|${title}|${teacher}`;
  const id=String(p.id||p.programId||p.afterId||p.afterSchoolId||p.activityId||p.masterId||'').trim();
  if(id) return `${group}|${id}`;
  return `${group}|${semester}|${title}|${teacher}`;
}
function inputCenterProgramCandidates(purpose=inputCenterConfig.purpose,semester=inputCenterConfig.semester){
  const filtered=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).filter(p=>{
    const group=inputCenterProgramGroup(p); if(!group) return false;
    const sem=inputCenterProgramSemester(p); if(sem&&semester&&sem!==String(semester)) return false;
    if(purpose==='보고서 연결') return (group==='common'||group==='selected') && p.reportRequired!==false && p.reportUse!==false;
    if(purpose==='프로그램 참가신청') return group==='selected'||group==='after'||group==='night';
    if(purpose==='만족도조사') return group==='common'||group==='selected'||group==='after'||group==='night';
    return false;
  });
  // readonly 프로그램 데이터에는 방과후 차시/일정 결합 때문에 같은 마스터 강좌가 여러 행으로 들어올 수 있습니다.
  // 입력센터 연결목록은 차시가 아니라 '프로그램 마스터 1건'을 선택하는 곳이므로 안정 ID 기준으로 1회만 노출합니다.
  const deduped=[]; const seen=new Set();
  filtered.forEach(p=>{const key=inputCenterProgramIdentity(p);if(seen.has(key))return;seen.add(key);deduped.push(p);});
  const rank={common:1,selected:2,after:3,night:4};
  return deduped.sort((a,b)=>(rank[inputCenterProgramGroup(a)]||9)-(rank[inputCenterProgramGroup(b)]||9)||String(a.actualTitle||a.title||'').localeCompare(String(b.actualTitle||b.title||'')));
}
function inputCenterProgramLabel(p={}){
  const g=inputCenterProgramGroup(p); const prefix={common:'공통',selected:'선택',after:'방과후',night:'야간심화'}[g]||'프로그램';
  const title=p.actualTitle||p.title||p.name||'';
  return `${prefix} · ${title}`;
}

function inputCenterBundleDuplex(files=[]){
  if(({'Excel·CSV':'교사 파일','문서 파일':'교사 파일'}[inputCenterConfig.source]||inputCenterConfig.source)!=='수기 스캔'||!['양면 스캔','단면 스캔','사진(JPG/PNG)'].includes(inputCenterConfig.subtype)) return files.flatMap(file=>inputCenterExpandTextFile(file));
  const per=Math.max(1,Number(inputCenterConfig.pagesPerStudent|| (inputCenterConfig.subtype==='양면 스캔'?2:1)));
  const out=[];
  for(let i=0;i<files.length;i+=per){
    const pair=files.slice(i,i+per); const first=pair[0]||{};
    const text=pair.map(x=>String(x.text||'')).filter(Boolean).join('\n\n');
    const draft=inputCenterDraftFromPayload(first,text);
    draft.bundleId=`BUNDLE-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8)}`;
    draft.pages=pair.map((x,j)=>({page:j+1,filePath:x.path||'',fileName:x.name||'',extension:x.extension||'',size:Number(x.size||0),rotation:0}));
    draft.pageCount=pair.length; draft.fileName=pair.map(x=>x.name).filter(Boolean).join(' + ')||(first.name||'스캔 원본');
    draft.matchStatus=draft.studentNo?draft.matchStatus:'판독 대기';
    draft.bundleStatus=pair.length===per?`${per}페이지 묶음 · 순서/회전 확인`:`예상 ${per}페이지 중 ${pair.length}페이지 · 확인 필요`;
    draft.batchScope=inputCenterConfig.batchScope; draft.batchGrade=inputCenterConfig.batchGrade; draft.batchClass=inputCenterConfig.batchClass;
    out.push(draft);
  }
  return out;
}

    return false;
  });
  // readonly 프로그램 데이터에는 방과후 차시/일정 결합 때문에 같은 마스터 강좌가 여러 행으로 들어올 수 있습니다.
  // 입력센터 연결목록은 차시가 아니라 '프로그램 마스터 1건'을 선택하는 곳이므로 안정 ID 기준으로 1회만 노출합니다.
  const deduped=[]; const seen=new Set();
  filtered.forEach(p=>{const key=inputCenterProgramIdentity(p);if(seen.has(key))return;seen.add(key);deduped.push(p);});
  const rank={common:1,selected:2,after:3,night:4};
  return deduped.sort((a,b)=>(rank[inputCenterProgramGroup(a)]||9)-(rank[inputCenterProgramGroup(b)]||9)||String(a.actualTitle||a.title||'').localeCompare(String(b.actualTitle||b.title||'')));
}
function inputCenterProgramLabel(p={}){
  const g=inputCenterProgramGroup(p); const prefix={common:'공통',selected:'선택',after:'방과후',night:'야간심화'}[g]||'프로그램';
  const title=p.actualTitle||p.title||p.name||'';
  return `${prefix} · ${title}`;
}

function studentCounselDetailMarkup(candidate,groupKey){
  if(!candidate)return `<section class="counsel-side-card empty"><div class="query-empty"><b>학생을 선택해 주세요.</b><span>목록에서 학생을 선택하면 현재 위치·충족 기준·부족한 부분·앞으로 준비할 내용을 이 화면에서 바로 확인할 수 있습니다.</span></div></section>`;
  const s=candidate.student, c=studentCounselCriteria(candidate,groupKey), base=candidate.base||{}, mock=candidate.mock||{}, prep=candidate.prep||{};
  const actual=Number.isFinite(Number(base.actual9))?Number(base.actual9).toFixed(2):'-';
  const avg5=Number.isFinite(Number(base.avg5))?Number(base.avg5).toFixed(2):'-';
  const groupName=groupKey==='seoul'?'인서울 관리군':groupKey==='regional'?'지거국 관리군':'일반 관리군';
  const mockRule=groupKey==='seoul'?'국·수·영 2개≤2, 전체≤3':groupKey==='regional'?'국·수·영 2개≤4, 전체≤5':'최근 모의 성적의 상승 흐름 확인';
  const row=(label,state,detail)=>`<div class="counsel-criterion-row"><span>${escapeHtml(label)}</span><i class="${studentCounselStatusClass(state)}">${studentCounselStatusLabel(state)}</i><b>${escapeHtml(detail)}</b></div>`;
  const guide=[];
  if(c.internal==='ok')guide.push(`현재 내신 9등급 환산 ${actual}로 ${groupName} 내신 기준을 충족하고 있습니다.`);
  else if(c.internal==='near')guide.push(`현재 내신 9등급 환산 ${actual}로 목표 기준에 근접해 있어 다음 학기 내신 유지·상승이 중요합니다.`);
  else guide.push(`현재 내신 9등급 환산 ${actual}이며 성적 향상 가능성과 강점 교과를 먼저 확인할 필요가 있습니다.`);
  if(c.mock!=='ok')guide.push(groupKey==='seoul'?'모의고사는 국·수·영 중 2개 영역 2등급 이내와 나머지 3등급 이내를 목표로 보완합니다.':groupKey==='regional'?'모의고사는 국·수·영 중 2개 영역 4등급 이내와 전체 5등급 이내를 우선 확인합니다.':'최근 모의고사에서 상승 가능한 영역을 정해 단기 목표를 세웁니다.');
  if(c.subjects!=='ok')guide.push('진로희망과 연결되는 선택과목 이수계획을 점검합니다.');
  if(c.activity!=='ok')guide.push('희망계열과 연결되는 선택활동과 프로그램 참여를 보완합니다.');
  if(c.reports!=='ok')guide.push('참여 활동을 보고서·탐구 결과로 남겨 학생부 연결성을 강화합니다.');
  const manage=(candidate.reasons||candidate.tags||[]).slice(0,3);
  return `<section class="counsel-side-card counsel-student-detail"><header><div><small>선택 학생 상세</small><h3>${escapeHtml(s.studentNo||'')} ${escapeHtml(s.name||'')} · ${escapeHtml(classNumberOf(s))}반</h3></div><button class="text-button" data-counsel-student-clear>학생 변경</button></header><div class="counsel-student-metrics"><span><small>내신 5등급</small><b>${avg5}</b><em>9등급 환산 ${actual}</em></span><span><small>최근 모의</small><b>${escapeHtml(mock.exam||'-')}</b><em>${escapeHtml(mock.detail||'자료 확인')}</em></span><span><small>대입관리</small><b>${escapeHtml(groupName)}</b><em>${c.internalLimit?`핵심선 ${c.internalLimit.toFixed(2)}`:'성장관리'}</em></span><span><small>관리포인트</small><b>${escapeHtml(manage[0]||'안정 추적')}</b><em>${escapeHtml(manage[1]||'학생부 연계 확인')}</em></span></div><div 진단</span><span>부족 분석</span><span>준비 방향</span></div><div class="counsel-guide-lines">${guide.map((g,i)=>`<p><i>${i+1}</i>${escapeHtml(g)}</p>`).join('')}</div><div class="counsel-next-actions"><b>다음 상담 }

function dormProgramIsHolisticTarget(program){
  return program?.kind==='selected'||(program?.kind==='common'&&program?.reportRequired===true);
}
function dormTermActivityStats(student,termMode){
  const view=studentActivityView(student);
  const order=['g1s1','g1s2','g2s1','g2s2','g3'];
  const cutoff=Math.max(0,order.indexOf(termMode));
  const allowed=new Set(order.slice(0,cutoff+1));
  const programs=(view.holisticPrograms||[]).filter(p=>allowed.has(dormProgramTermKey(p)));
  const canonical=studentCanonicalReports(student);
  const reportTargets=programs.filter(p=>programRequiresStudentReport(p,student,canonical));
  const reports=[...new Map(reportTargets.map(p=>resolveStudentProgramReport(p,student,canonical)).filter(Boolean).map(r=>[canonicalReportKey(r),r])).values()];
  return {programs,reportTargets,reports,submitted:reports.length,reportRate:reportTargets.length?Math.min(100,reports.length/reportTargets.length*100):0};
}
function dormCohortAverage(termMode){
  const roster=dormTermRoster(termMode);
  const stats=roster.map(row=>{
    const student=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(s=>String(s.id)===String(row.studentId)||String(s.studentNo)===String(row.studentNo));
    return student?dormTermActivityStats(student,termMode):null;
  }).filter(Boolean);
  const avg=(key)=>stats.length?stats.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+Number(x[key]||0),0)/stats.length:0;
  return {count:stats.length,programs:stats.length?stats.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+x.programs.length,0)/stats.length:0,reportTargets:stats.length?stats.reduce((sum,x)=>sum+(x.reportTargets?.length||0),0)/stats.length:0,submitted:avg('submitted'),reportRate:avg('reportRate')};
}
function dormProgramActivityMarkup(student,termMode,bundle){
  const termStats=dormTermActivityStats(student,termMode),cohort=dormCohortAverage(termMode);

  const after=allAfter.filter(p=>!night.includes(p));
  const uniqueTitles=rows=>[...new Set(rows.map(p=>String(p.actualTitle||p.title||'').trim()).filter(Boolean))];
  const attendance=(readonlyCache?.nightAttendance||[]).filter(r=>studentMatches(r,student));
  const programRows=termStats.programs.map(p=>{
    const report=p.report||resolveStudentProgramReport(p,student,termStats.reports);
    const submitted=Boolean(report&&report.submitted!==false);return `<li class="${submitted?'has-report':''}"><b>${escapeHtml(p.recordTitle||p.actualTitle||p.title||'프로그램')}</b>${submitted?`<button type="button" class="state positive dorm-report-open" data-dorm-report-id="${escapeHtml(report.id||'')}" data-dorm-report-student="${escapeHtml(student.id||'')}">보고서 제출 · 보기</button>`:`<span class="state warn">보고서 미제출</span>`}</li>`;
  }).join('');
  return `<div class="dorm-activity-grid"><article><h4>${escapeHtml(dormTermDefinition(termMode)?.label||'현재')} 프로그램·보고서</h4><div class="dorm-cohort-compare"><span><small>본인 프로그램</small><b>${termStats.programs.length}</b></span><span><small>학사생 평균</small><b>${cohort.programs.toFixed(1)}</b></span><span><small>본인 보고서율</small><b>${Math.round(termStats.reportRate)}%</b><em>${termStats.submitted}/${termStats.reportTargets?.length||0}</em></span><span><small>학사생 평균</small><b>${Math.round(cohort.reportRate)}%</b><em>대상 ${cohort.reportTargets.toFixed(1)}</em></span></div><ul>${programRows||'<li class="empty-inline">해당 학기 프로그램 자료 없음</li>'}</ul></article><article><h4>방과후·야간심화 근황</h4><p><b>방과후</b> ${escapeHtml(uniqueTitles(after).join(' · ')||'참여 기록 없음')}</p><p><b>야간심화</b> ${escapeHtml(uniqueTitles(night).join(' · ')||'참여 기록 없음')}</p><p><b>야자 출석기록</b> ${attendance.length}건</p></article></div>`;
}
function dormInseoulGap(student){
  const row=admissionManagementDataset('inseoul').find(x=>String(x.student?.id||'')===String(student.id||''));
  const standard=row?.standard||{internal:uepManagementRule('inseoul').internal,mockText:uepManagementRule('inseoul').mockText};
  const actual=row?.base?.actual9;
  const internalGap=actual==null?null:Math.max(0,(actual-Number(standard.internal))/Number(standard.internal)*100);
  const levels=(row?.mock?.mainLevels||[]).filter(Number.isFinite);
  const within3=levels.filter(v=>v<=3).length,within2=levels.filter(v=>v<=2).length;
  const mockScore=levels.length?Math.min(100,((Math.min(within3,3)+Math.min(within2,2))/5)*100):null;
  const mockGap=mockScore==null?null:100-mockScore;
  const overall=[internalGap,mockGap].filter(v=>v!=null).length?Math.max(...[internalGap,mockGap].filter(v=>v!=null)):null;
  const gaps=[];
  if(actual==null)gaps.push('내신 9등급 환산자료 확인 필요');
  else if(actual>standard.internal)gaps.push(`내신 ${actual.toFixed(2)} → 핵심기준 ${Number(standard.internal).toFixed(2)} 이내 보완`);
  else gaps.push(`내신 핵심기준 충족 (${actual.toFixed(2)})`);
  if(levels.length<3)gaps.push('국·수·영 모의고사 자료 보완');
  else{
    levels.forEach((v,i)=>{if(v>3)gaps.push(`${['국어','수학','영어'][i]||'주요영역'} ${v}등급 → 3등급 이내`);});
    if(within2<2)gaps.push(`국·수·영 2등급 이내 ${within2}개 → 2개 필요`);
    if(within3===3&&within2>=2)gaps.push('모의고사 인서울 핵심기준 충족');
  }
  return {row,standard,actual,internalGap,mockGap,overall,gaps};
}
function dormComprehensiveCounselMarkup(student,termMode,bundle){
  const gap=dormInseoulGap(student),career=studentCareerSupport(student,bundle),stats=dormTermActivityStats(student,termMode),cohort=dormCohortAverage(termMode);
  const selected=(bundle.selected||[]).filter(r=>String(r.final||'Y')!=='N');
  const points=[...gap.gaps];
  if(!selected.length)points.push('희망계열에 맞는 선택과목 이수계획 확인 필요');
  if(stats.programs.length<cohort.programs)points.push(`계열 연계 프로그램: 본인 ${stats.programs.length}개 / 학사생 평균 ${cohort.programs.toFixed(1)}개`);
  if(stats.reportRate+1<cohort.reportRate)points.push(`보고서 제출률: 본인 ${Math.round(stats.reportRate)}% / 학사생 평균 ${Math.round(cohort.reportRate)}%`);
  points.push(`진로희망 ${career.hopeTrack} · ${career.hopeMajor}와 선택과목·탐구활동의 연속성 확인`);
  return `<section class="dorm-counsel-priority"><header><div><small>SEOUL · CORE GAP</small><h4>인서울 핵심관리 괴리 최우선</h4></div><strong>${gap.overall==null?'자료 확인':gap.overall<=0?'핵심기준 충족':`최대 괴리 ${gap.overall.toFixed(0)}%`}</strong></header><div class="dorm-gap-metrics"><span><small>내신 핵심기준</small><b>9등급 ${Number(gap.standard.internal).toFixed(2)} 이내</b></span><span><small>현재 내신</small><b>${gap.actual==null?'-':gap.actual.toFixed(2)}</b><em>${gap.internalGap==null?'자료 없음':gap.internalGap===0?'충족':`괴리 +${gap.internalGap.toFixed(0)}%`}</em></span><span><small>모의 핵심기준</small><b>국·수·영 2개≤2, 전체≤3</b></span><span><small>모의 충족 괴리</small><b>${gap.mockGap==null?'-':`${gap.mockGap.toFixed(0)}%`}</b></span></div><ol>${points.map(p=>`<li>${escapeHtml(p)}</li>`).join('')}</ol><p class="dorm-holistic-note"><b>학생부종합전형 관점</b> 성적만으로 순위를 매기지 않고 진로희망–선택과목–계열연계 프로그램–보고서 탐구의 연결성과 지속성을 함께 상담합니다.</p></section>`;
}

function timetableSubjectStyle(subject) {
  const text=String(subject||"").replace(/\s+/g,"");
  const rules=[
    [/국어|한문/,"subject-korean"],[/수학/,"subject-math"],[/영어/,"subject-english"],
    [/사회|한국사|역사|지리|윤리/,"subject-social"],[/과학|물리|화학|생명|지구/,"subject-science"],
    [/진로|동아리|자율|창체/,"subject-creative"],[/체육|음악|미술/,"subject-art"]
  ];
  return rules.find(([pattern])=>pattern.test(text))?.[1]||"subject-other";
}

function shiftDateKey(key, delta) {
  /* UEP_08197_ATTENDANCE_DATE_SHIFT */
  const match = String(key || "").match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
  if (!match) return dateKey(today);
  const year = Number(match[1]), month = Number(match[2]), day = Number(match[3]);
  const date = new Date(year, month - 1, day + Number(delta || 0), 12, 0, 0, 0);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return dateKey(today);
  return date.getFullYear() + "-" + String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0") + "-" + String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
}
function attendanceDateControls(label,actionHtml="") {
  return `<div class="attendance-date-controls"><span>${escapeHtml(label)}</span><button class="btn secondary" data-attendance-date-step="-1">‹</button><div class="attendance-date-input-wrap"><input type="date" id="attendanceViewDate" value="${escapeHtml(attendanceViewDate)}"><strong>${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(attendanceViewDate, true))}</strong></div><button class="btn secondary" data-attendance-date-step="1">›</button><button class="btn secondary" data-attendance-today>오늘</button>${actionHtml?`<div class="attendance-date-action">${actionHtml}</div>`:""}</div>`;
}
function nightSlotName(rowOrText) {
  const text = typeof rowOrText === "string" ? rowOrText : `${rowOrText?.time || ""} ${rowOrText?.type || ""} ${rowOrText?.detail || ""}`;
  // 운호고 방과후 8교시는 오후자습 시간대이다.
  if (/오후자습|1타임|8교시/.test(text)) return "오후자습";
  if (/야자\s*1|야간\s*1|2타임/.test(text)) return "야자1";
  if (/야자\s*2|야간\s*2|3타임/.test(text)) return "야자2";
  return String(rowOrText?.time || rowOrText?.type || text || "기타").trim();
}
function minutesOfClock(value) {
  const match = String(value || "").match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  return match ? Number(match[1]) * 60 + Number(match[2]) : null;
}
function slotRange(slot) {
  return {"오후자습":[17*60,18*60],"야자1":[19*60+20,20*60+10],"야자2":[20*60+20,21*60+30]}[slot] || null;
}
function isWednesdayKey(value) {
  const key=String(value||"").slice(0,10);
  if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(key)){
    const d=new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
    return !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())&&d.getDay()===3;
  }
  return /^수(?:요일)?$/.test(String(value||"").trim());
}
function programHasNightStudyImpact(program) {
  const dates=[program?.date,...(Array.isArray(program?.dates)?program.dates:[])].filter(Boolean);
  if(dates.length&&dates.every(isWednesdayKey)) return false;
  const weekdayText=String(program?.weekdays||program?.weekday||program?.day||"").replace(/\s/g,"");
  if(weekdayText&&/^(수|수요일)$/.test(weekdayText)) return false;
  // UEP_08177_AFTER_SCHOOL_NIGHT_LINK_ONLY: 문자열 N을 Boolean(N)으로 처리하지 않는다.
  const raw=program?.affectsAttendance??program?.nightLinked??program?.nightLink??program?.['야자연계여부'];
  if(raw===true)return true;
  if(raw===false||raw==null)return false;
  return /^(Y|YES|TRUE|1|예|연계|적용)$/i.test(String(raw).trim());
}
function singlePeriodLabel(session) {
  const candidates=[session?.period,session?.startTime,session?.time].map(v=>String(v||"").trim()).filter(Boolean);
  for(const text of candidates){
    const m=text.match(/(\d+)교시/);
    if(m) return `${m[1]}교시`;
  }
  return String(session?.time||session?.period||"시간 확인");
}
function programOverlapsSlot(program, slot) {
  if(isWednesdayKey(program?.date)||!programHasNightStudyImpact(program)) return false;
  const descriptor = `${program?.time || ""} ${program?.title || ""} ${program?.actualTitle || ""} ${program?.type || ""}`;
  // 차시 시작시각이 8교시라면 시트의 기존 야자영향타임 값보다 학교 운영 기준을 우선한다.
  // 즉 8교시는 야자1이 아니라 오후자습으로만 표시한다.
  if (/8교시/.test(descriptor)) return slot === "오후자습";
  const explicitSlots=(program?.nightSlots||[]).flatMap(value=>{
    const text=String(value||"");
    return ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].filter(name=>text.includes(name));
  });
  if(explicitSlots.length) return explicitSlots.includes(slot);
  const range = slotRange(slot);
  const namedSlot = nightSlotName(descriptor);
  if (namedSlot && ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].includes(namedSlot) && !String(namedSlot).includes("기타")) return namedSlot === slot;
  const clocks = descriptor.match(/\d{1,2}:\d{2}/g) || [];
  if (!range || clocks.length < 2) return Boolean(program?.affectsAttendance && /오후자습/.test(descriptor) && slot === "오후자습");
  const start = minutesOfClock(clocks[0]), end = minutesOfClock(clocks[1]);
  return Number.isFinite(start) && Number.isFinite(end) && start < range[1] && end > range[0];
}
function programParticipationFor(student, day, slot) {
  const id = String(student?.id || student?.studentId || "");
  const number = String(student?.studentNo || "");
  const connected = readonlyCache?.programs || [];
  const derived = typeof afterSchoolProgramGroups === "function" ? afterSchoolProgramGroups() : [];
  const seen = new Set();
  const programs = [...connected, ...derived].filter(program => {
    const key = `${program.id || ""}|${program.date || ""}|${program.title || ""}|${program.time || ""}`;
    if (seen.has(key)) return false; seen.add(key); return true;
  });
  for (const program of programs) {
    if(isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program))continue;
    if (program.date !== day || !programOverlapsSlot(program, slot)) continue;
    const target = (program.students || []).find(item => String(item.studentId || "") === id || (number && String(item.studentNo || item.number || "") === number));
    if (!target) continue;
    const participationText = [target.result,target.attendance,target.participation,target.status,target.checkMethod].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
    const inactive = /결석|불참|취소|중도포기|퇴강|미참여/.test(participationText);
    // 13_출석부의 활성 수강명단은 출석상태가 '등록'이어도 해당 실제 차시에는 프로그램 참여자로 본다.
    // 실제 수업일·야자영향타임은 program.date/program.time 단계에서 이미 검증되므로 명단 등록만으로 다른 날짜에 표시되지 않는다.
    const confirmed = !inactive && ((typeof nightRecordIsAttendance === "function" && nightRecordIsAttendance(target)) || /출석|정상|참여|입실|명단QR|학사|등록|신청/.test(participationText));
    return {program, target, confirmed, pending: !confirmed && !inactive};
  }
  return null;
}

const NIGHT_STUDY_RULES_STORE='uep-night-study-rules-v1';
function defaultNightStudyRules(){
  return [
    {id:'weekly-wed',name:'수요일 미운영',type:'weekday',weekday:3,active:true},
    {id:'summer-2026',name:'2026 여름방학 미운영',type:'range',start:'2026-08-08',end:'2026-08-17',active:true}
  ];
}
function readNightStudyRules(){
  try{const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(NIGHT_STUDY_RULES_STORE)||'null');return Array.isArray(saved)?saved:defaultNightStudyRules();}
  catch{return defaultNightStudyRules();}
}
function nightStudyOperationStatus(key){
  const date=new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
  const rules=readNightStudyRules().filter(r=>r&&r.active!==false);
  for(const r of rules){
    if(r.type==='range'&&r.start&&r.end&&key>=r.start&&key<=r.end)return {operates:false,reason:r.name||'미운영 기간'};
    if(r.type==='weekday'&&Number(r.weekday)===date.getDay())return {operates:false,reason:r.name||'요일 미운영'};
  }
  return {operates:true,reason:'운영일'};
}

    const rows = liveStudents.map((student, i) => `<button class="student-detail-card student-row hidden ${studentHeatClass(student)}" data-student="${i}" data-class="${escapeHtml(classNumberOf(student))}" data-search="${escapeHtml([student.studentNo, student.name, student.officialName, student.displayName, student.className, student.middleSchool].join(" ").toLowerCase())}" data-status="${escapeHtml(student.status || "재학")}"><header><b>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo || "-")} ${escapeHtml(student.name)}</b><em>상세 ›</em></header><div><span><small>학적</small>${escapeHtml(student.status || "재학")}</span><span><small>학사</small>${escapeHtml(student.dorm || "-")}</span><span><small>NFC</small>${escapeHtml(student.nfc || "-")}</span></div><footer>${escapeHtml(student.middleSchool || "-")}</footer></button>`).join("");
    return `<div class="module-page student-master-page student-master-compact">${studentPageTabs}<div class="student-class-dashboard">${classCards}</div><div class="toolbar student-master-toolbar"><input class="search" type="search" id="studentMasterSearch" placeholder="학번·이름·반·출신중학교 검색"><select class="filter" id="studentStatusFilter"><option value="">전체 학적</option><option>재학</option><option>전입</option><option>전출</option><option>자퇴</option></select><div class="student-heat-period" title="학생카드 색은 선택 기간 안의 클릭 횟수로 계산됩니다."><small>카드색 기준</small><button type="button" data-student-heat-period="7" class="${studentCardHeatPeriodDays()===7?'active':''}">최근 7일</button><button type="button" data-student-heat-period="30" class="${studentCardHeatPeriodDays()===30?'active':''}">최근 30일</button><button type="button" data-student-heat-reset>색 초기화</button></div><span class="state" id="studentSelectionState">반을 선택하세요</span></div><div class="student-master-table hidden" id="studentMasterTable"><div id="studentMasterRows" class="student-card-grid">${rows}</div><div class="empty-state hidden" id="studentSearchEmpty"><b>일치하는 학생이 없습니다.</b><span>학번·성명·반 또는 학적 조건을 다시 확인하세요.</span></div></div></div>`;
  }
  return `<div class="module-page"><div class="empty-state data-card"><b>학생정보를 불러오지 못했습니다.</b><span>설정에서 Google 연결 인증을 등록한 뒤 새로고침하세요.</span></div></div>`;
}


const NIGHT_STUDY_RULES_STORE='uep-night-study-rules-v1';
function defaultNightStudyRules(){
  return [
    {id:'weekly-wed',name:'수요일 미운영',type:'weekday',weekday:3,active:true},
    {id:'summer-2026',name:'2026 여름방학 미운영',type:'range',start:'2026-08-08',end:'2026-08-17',active:true}
  ];
}
function readNightStudyRules(){
  try{const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(NIGHT_STUDY_RULES_STORE)||'null');return Array.isArray(saved)?saved:defaultNightStudyRules();}
  catch{return defaultNightStudyRules();}
}
function nightStudyOperationStatus(key){
  const date=new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
  const rules=readNightStudyRules().filter(r=>r&&r.active!==false);
  for(const r of rules){
    if(r.type==='range'&&r.start&&r.end&&key>=r.start&&key<=r.end)return {operates:false,reason:r.name||'미운영 기간'};
    if(r.type==='weekday'&&Number(r.weekday)===date.getDay())return {operates:false,reason:r.name||'요일 미운영'};
  }
  return {operates:true,reason:'운영일'};
}
