    return `<div class="module-page student-master-page student-master-compact">${studentPageTabs}<div class="student-class-dashboard">${classCards}</div><div class="toolbar student-master-toolbar"><input class="search" type="search" id="studentMasterSearch" placeholder="학번·이름·반·출신중학교 검색"><select class="filter" id="studentStatusFilter"><option value="">전체 학적</option><option>재학</option><option>전입</option><option>전출</option><option>자퇴</option></select><div class="student-heat-period" title="학생카드 색은 선택 기간 안의 클릭 횟수로 계산됩니다."><small>카드색 기준</small><button type="button" data-student-heat-period="7" class="${studentCardHeatPeriodDays()===7?'active':''}">최근 7일</button><button type="button" data-student-heat-period="30" class="${studentCardHeatPeriodDays()===30?'active':''}">최근 30일</button><button type="button" data-student-heat-reset>색 초기화</button></div><span class="state" id="studentSelectionState">반을 선택하세요</span></div><div class="student-master-table hidden" id="studentMasterTable"><div id="studentMasterRows" class="student-card-grid">${rows}</div><div class="empty-state hidden" id="studentSearchEmpty"><b>일치하는 학생이 없습니다.</b><span>학번·성명·반 또는 학적 조건을 다시 확인하세요.</span></div></div></div>`;
  }
  return `<div class="module-page"><div class="empty-state data-card"><b>학생정보를 불러오지 못했습니다.</b><span>설정에서 Google 연결 인증을 등록한 뒤 새로고침하세요.</span></div></div>`;
}


const NIGHT_STUDY_RULES_STORE='uep-night-study-rules-v1';
function defaultNightStudyRules(){
  return [
    {id:'weekly-wed',name:'수요일 미운영',type:'weekday',weekday:3,active:true},
    {id:'summer-2026',name:'2026 여름방학 미운영',type:'range',start:'2026-08-08',end:'2026-08-17',active:true}
  ];
}
function readNightStudyRules(){
  try{const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(NIGHT_STUDY_RULES_STORE)||'null');return Array.isArray(saved)?saved:defaultNightStudyRules();}
  catch{return defaultNightStudyRules();}
}
function nightStudyOperationStatus(key){
  const date=new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
  const rules=readNightStudyRules().filter(r=>r&&r.active!==false);
  for(const r of rules){
    if(r.type==='range'&&r.start&&r.end&&key>=r.start&&key<=r.end)return {operates:false,reason:r.name||'미운영 기간'};
    if(r.type==='weekday'&&Number(r.weekday)===date.getDay())return {operates:false,reason:r.name||'요일 미운영'};
  }
  return {operates:true,reason:'운영일'};
}

    return `<div class="module-page student-master-page student-master-compact">${studentPageTabs}<div class="student-class-dashboard">${classCards}</div><div class="toolbar student-master-toolbar"><input class="search" type="search" id="studentMasterSearch" placeholder="학번·이름·반·출신중학교 검색"><select class="filter" id="studentStatusFilter"><option value="">전체 학적</option><option>재학</option><option>전입</option><option>전출</option><option>자퇴</option></select><div class="student-heat-period" title="학생카드 색은 선택 기간 안의 클릭 횟수로 계산됩니다."><small>카드색 기준</small><button type="button" data-student-heat-period="7" class="${studentCardHeatPeriodDays()===7?'active':''}">최근 7일</button><button type="button" data-student-heat-period="30" class="${studentCardHeatPeriodDays()===30?'active':''}">최근 30일</button><button type="button" data-student-heat-reset>색 초기화</button></div><span class="state" id="studentSelectionState">반을 선택하세요</span></div><div class="student-master-table hidden" id="studentMasterTable"><div id="studentMasterRows" class="student-card-grid">${rows}</div><div class="empty-state hidden" id="studentSearchEmpty"><b>일치하는 학생이 없습니다.</b><span>학번·성명·반 또는 학적 조건을 다시 확인하세요.</span></div></div></div>`;
  }
  return `<div class="module-page"><div class="empty-state data-card"><b>학생정보를 불러오지 못했습니다.</b><span>설정에서 Google 연결 인증을 등록한 뒤 새로고침하세요.</span></div></div>`;
}


const NIGHT_STUDY_RULES_STORE='uep-night-study-rules-v1';
function defaultNightStudyRules(){
  return [
    {id:'weekly-wed',name:'수요일 미운영',type:'weekday',weekday:3,active:true},
    {id:'summer-2026',name:'2026 여름방학 미운영',type:'range',start:'2026-08-08',end:'2026-08-17',active:true}
  ];
}
function readNightStudyRules(){
  try{const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(NIGHT_STUDY_RULES_STORE)||'null');return Array.isArray(saved)?saved:defaultNightStudyRules();}
  catch{return defaultNightStudyRules();}
}
function nightStudyOperationStatus(key){
  const date=new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
  const rules=readNightStudyRules().filter(r=>r&&r.active!==false);
  for(const r of rules){
    if(r.type==='range'&&r.start&&r.end&&key>=r.start&&key<=r.end)return {operates:false,reason:r.name||'미운영 기간'};
    if(r.type==='weekday'&&Number(r.weekday)===date.getDay())return {operates:false,reason:r.name||'요일 미운영'};
  }
  return {operates:true,reason:'운영일'};
}

function timetableClassStyle(label) {
  const text = String(label || "").trim();
  if (!text || text === "-" || !/(?:^|\D)[123]-?\d(?:\D|$)|\d반/.test(text)) return { cls:"", style:"" };
  const palette = [
    ["#e9f6ff","#63a9d8","#174f72"], ["#eef7e8","#78ad61","#315d28"],
    ["#fff2e8","#dd9362","#7a4523"], ["#f3ecff","#9b79d0","#50367a"],
    ["#e8f8f4","#57ab98","#245e52"], ["#fff0f4","#d9829b","#77384b"],
    ["#f7f3df","#b8a75b","#665a21"], ["#eaf0ff","#718ed2","#354c86"],
    ["#f0f4f7","#879caf","#425568"]
  ];
  let hash = 0; for (const ch of text) hash = (hash * 31 + ch.charCodeAt(0)) >>> 0;
  const [fill,border,color] = palette[hash % palette.length];
  return { cls:"class-colored", style:` style="--class-fill:${fill};--class-border:${border};--class-text:${color}"` };
}
function timetableSubjectStyle(subject) {
  const text=String(subject||"").replace(/\s+/g,"");
  const rules=[
    [/국어|한문/,"subject-korean"],[/수학/,"subject-math"],[/영어/,"subject-english"],
    [/사회|한국사|역사|지리|윤리/,"subject-social"],[/과학|물리|화학|생명|지구/,"subject-science"],
    [/진로|동아리|자율|창체/,"subject-creative"],[/체육|음악|미술/,"subject-art"]
  ];
  return rules.find(([pattern])=>pattern.test(text))?.[1]||"subject-other";
}

function shiftDateKey(key, delta) {
  /* UEP_08197_ATTENDANCE_DATE_SHIFT */
  const match = String(key || "").match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
  if (!match) return dateKey(today);
  const year = Number(match[1]), month = Number(match[2]), day = Number(match[3]);
  const date = new Date(year, month - 1, day + Number(delta || 0), 12, 0, 0, 0);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return dateKey(today);
  return date.getFullYear() + "-" + String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0") + "-" + String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
}
function attendanceDateControls(label,actionHtml="") {
  return `<div class="attendance-date-controls"><span>${escapeHtml(label)}</span><button class="btn secondary" data-attendance-date-step="-1">‹</button><div class="attendance-date-input-wrap"><input type="date" id="attendanceViewDate" value="${escapeHtml(attendanceViewDate)}"><strong>${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(attendanceViewDate, true))}</strong></div><button class="btn secondary" data-attendance-date-step="1">›</button><button class="btn secondary" data-attendance-today>오늘</button>${actionHtml?`<div class="attendance-date-action">${actionHtml}</div>`:""}</div>`;
}
function nightSlotName(rowOrText) {
  const text = typeof rowOrText === "string" ? rowOrText : `${rowOrText?.time || ""} ${rowOrText?.type || ""} ${rowOrText?.detail || ""}`;
  // 운호고 방과후 8교시는 오후자습 시간대이다.
  if (/오후자습|1타임|8교시/.test(text)) return "오후자습";
  if (/야자\s*1|야간\s*1|2타임/.test(text)) return "야자1";
  if (/야자\s*2|야간\s*2|3타임/.test(text)) return "야자2";
  return String(rowOrText?.time || rowOrText?.type || text || "기타").trim();
}
function minutesOfClock(value) {
  const match = String(value || "").match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  return match ? Number(match[1]) * 60 + Number(match[2]) : null;
}
function slotRange(slot) {
  return {"오후자습":[17*60,18*60],"야자1":[19*60+20,20*60+10],"야자2":[20*60+20,21*60+30]}[slot] || null;
}
function isWednesdayKey(value) {
  const key=String(value||"").slice(0,10);
  if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(key)){
    const d=new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
    return !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())&&d.getDay()===3;
  }
  return /^수(?:요일)?$/.test(String(value||"").trim());
}
function programHasNightStudyImpact(program) {
  const dates=[program?.date,...(Array.isArray(program?.dates)?program.dates:[])].filter(Boolean);
  if(dates.length&&dates.every(isWednesdayKey)) return false;
  const weekdayText=String(program?.weekdays||program?.weekday||program?.day||"").replace(/\s/g,"");
  if(weekdayText&&/^(수|수요일)$/.test(weekdayText)) return false;
  // UEP_08177_AFTER_SCHOOL_NIGHT_LINK_ONLY: 문자열 N을 Boolean(N)으로 처리하지 않는다.
  const raw=program?.affectsAttendance??program?.nightLinked??program?.nightLink??program?.['야자연계여부'];
  if(raw===true)return true;
  if(raw===false||raw==null)return false;
  return /^(Y|YES|TRUE|1|예|연계|적용)$/i.test(String(raw).trim());
}
function singlePeriodLabel(session) {
  const candidates=[session?.period,session?.startTime,session?.time].map(v=>String(v||"").trim()).filter(Boolean);
  for(const text of candidates){
    const m=text.match(/(\d+)교시/);
    if(m) return `${m[1]}교시`;
  }
  return String(session?.time||session?.period||"시간 확인");
}
function programOverlapsSlot(program, slot) {
  if(isWednesdayKey(program?.date)||!programHasNightStudyImpact(program)) return false;
  const descriptor = `${program?.time || ""} ${program?.title || ""} ${program?.actualTitle || ""} ${program?.type || ""}`;
  // 차시 시작시각이 8교시라면 시트의 기존 야자영향타임 값보다 학교 운영 기준을 우선한다.
  // 즉 8교시는 야자1이 아니라 오후자습으로만 표시한다.
  if (/8교시/.test(descriptor)) return slot === "오후자습";
  const explicitSlots=(program?.nightSlots||[]).flatMap(value=>{
    const text=String(value||"");
    return ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].filter(name=>text.includes(name));
  });
  if(explicitSlots.length) return explicitSlots.includes(slot);
  const range = slotRange(slot);
  const namedSlot = nightSlotName(descriptor);
  if (namedSlot && ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].includes(namedSlot) && !String(namedSlot).includes("기타")) return namedSlot === slot;
  const clocks = descriptor.match(/\d{1,2}:\d{2}/g) || [];
  if (!range || clocks.length < 2) return Boolean(program?.affectsAttendance && /오후자습/.test(descriptor) && slot === "오후자습");
  const start = minutesOfClock(clocks[0]), end = minutesOfClock(clocks[1]);
  return Number.isFinite(start) && Number.isFinite(end) && start < range[1] && end > range[0];
}
function programParticipationFor(student, day, slot) {
  const id = String(student?.id || student?.studentId || "");

    if (seen.has(key)) return false; seen.add(key); return true;
  });
  for (const program of programs) {
    if(isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program))continue;
    if (program.date !== day || !programOverlapsSlot(program, slot)) continue;

    if (!target) continue;
    const participationText = [target.result,target.attendance,target.participation,target.status,target.checkMethod].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
    const inactive = /결석|불참|취소|중도포기|퇴강|미참여/.test(participationText);
    // 13_출석부의 활성 수강명단은 출석상태가 '등록'이어도 해당 실제 차시에는 프로그램 참여자로 본다.
    // 실제 수업일·야자영향타임은 program.date/program.time 단계에서 이미 검증되므로 명단 등록만으로 다른 날짜에 표시되지 않는다.
    const conf
--- match 8 at 182749 ---

    [/진로|동아리|자율|창체/,"subject-creative"],[/체육|음악|미술/,"subject-art"]
  ];

}

function shiftDateKey(key, delta) {
  /* UEP_08197_ATTENDANCE_DATE_SHIFT */
  const match = String(key || "").match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
  if (!match) return dateKey(today);
  const year = Number(match[1]), month = Number(match[2]), day = Number(match[3]);
  const date = new Date(year, month - 1, day + Number(delta || 0), 12, 0, 0, 0);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return dateKey(today);
  return date.getFullYear() + "-" + String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0") + "-" + String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
}

function programHasNightStudyImpact(program) {
  const dates=[program?.date,...(Array.isArray(program?.dates)?program.dates:[])].filter(Boolean);
  if(dates.length&&dates.every(isWednesdayKey)) return false;
  const weekdayText=String(program?.weekdays||program?.weekday||program?.day||"").replace(/\s/g,"");
  if(weekdayText&&/^(수|수요일)$/.test(weekdayText)) return false;
  // UEP_08177_AFTER_SCHOOL_NIGHT_LINK_ONLY: 문자열 N을 Boolean(N)으로 처리하지 않는다.
  const raw=program?.affectsAttendance??program?.nightLinked??program?.nightLink??program?.['야자연계여부'];
  if(raw===true)return true;
  if(raw===false||raw==null)return false;
  return /^(Y|YES|TRUE|1|예|연계|적용)$/i.test(String(raw).trim());
}
function singlePeriodLabel(session) {
  const candidates=[session?.period,session?.startTime,session?.time].map(v=>String(v||"").trim()).filter(Boolean);
  for(const text of candidates){
    const m=text.match(/(\d+)교시/);
    if(m) return `${m[1]}교시`;
  }
  return String(session?.time||session?.period||"시간 확인");
}
function programOverlapsSlot(program, slot) {
  if(isWednesdayKey(program?.date)||!programHasNightStudyImpact(program)) return false;
  const descriptor = `${program?.time || ""} ${program?.title || ""} ${program?.actualTitle || ""} ${program?.type || ""}`;
  // 차시 시작시각이 8교시라면 시트의 기존 야자영향타임 값보다 학교 운영 기준을 우선한다.
  // 즉 8교시는 야자1이 아니라 오후자습으로만 표시한다.
  if (/8교시/.test(descriptor)) return slot === "오후자습";
  const explicitSlots=(program?.nightSlots||[]).flatMap(value=>{
    const text=String(value||"");
    return ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].filter(name=>text.includes(name));
  });
  if(explicitSlots.length) return explicitSlots.includes(slot);
  const range = slotRange(slot);
  const namedSlot = nightSlotName(descriptor);
  if (namedSlot && ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].includes(namedSlot) && !String(namedSlot).includes("기타")) return namedSlot === slot;
  const clocks = descriptor.match(/\d{1,2}:\d{2}/g) || [];
  if (!range || clocks.length < 2) return Boolean(program?.affectsAttendance && /오후자습/.test(descriptor) && slot === "오후자습");
  const start = minutesOfClock(clocks[0]), end = minutesOfClock(clocks[1]);
  return Number.isFinite(start) && Number.isFinite(end) && start < range[1] && end > range[0];
}
function programParticipationFor(student, day, slot) {
  const id = String(student?.id || student?.studentId || "");
  const number = String(student?.studentNo || "");
  const connected = readonlyCache?.programs || [];
  const derived = typeof afterSchoolProgramGroups === "function" ? afterSchoolProgramGroups() : [];
  const seen = new Set();
  const programs = [...connected, ...derived].filter(program => {
    const key = `${program.id || ""}|${program.date || ""}|${program.title || ""}|${program.time || ""}`;
    if (seen.has(key)) return false; seen.add(key); return true;
  });
  for (const program of programs) {
    if(isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program))continue;
    if (program.date !== day || !programOverlapsSlot(program, slot)) continue;
    const target = (program.students || []).find(item => String(item.studentId || "") === id || (number && String(item.studentNo || item.number || "") === number));
    if (!target) continue;
    const participationText = [target.result,target.attendance,target.participation,target.status,target.checkMethod].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
    const inactive = /결석|불참|취소|중도포기|퇴강|미참여/.test(participationText);
    // 13_출석부의 활성 수강명단은 출석상태가 '등록'이어도 해당 실제 차시에는 프로그램 참여자로 본다.
    // 실제 수업일·야자영향타임은 program.date/program.time 단계에서 이미 검증되므로 명단 등록만으로 다른 날짜에 표시되지 않는다.
    const confirmed = !inactive && ((typeof nightRecordIsAttendance === "function" && nightRecordIsAttendance(target)) || /출석|정상|참여|입실|명단QR|학사|등록|신청/.test(participationText));
    return {program, target, confirmed, pending: !confirmed && !inactive};
  }
  return null;
}
function nightProgramStatus(program){
  const text=`${program?.afterType||""} ${program?.type||""} ${program?.actualTitle||program?.title||""}`;
  if(/야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)) return {cls:"after",label:"야간심화",kind:"after"};
  if(/일반 방과후|방학 방과후|방과후학교/.test(text)) return {cls:"after",label:"방과후",kind:"after"};
  const title=String(program?.actualTitle||program?.title||"프로그램");
  return {cls:"program",label:title.length>8?`${title.slice(0,7)}…`:title,kind:program?.kind||"general"};
}
function attendanceIsWeekday(day){const n=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`).getDay();return n>=1&&n<=5;}

  const classCards=`<div class="night-class-overview daily-class-overview">${classSummaries.map(item=>`<button data-night-class="${item.classNo}" class="${classNo===item.classNo?"active":""}"><small>${item.classNo}반 · 재적 ${item.roster}명</small><b>${item.participants}<em>당일 참여</em></b><span>출석체크 ${item.checks}건</span></button>`).join("")}</div>`;

  const dateHead=dates.map(day=>`<th colspan="3" class="${day===attendanceViewDate?"selected-day":""}" data-night-date="${escapeHtml(day)}"><b>${escapeHtml(day.slice(5).replace("-","/"))}</b><small>${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(day))}</small></th>`).join("");
  const slotHead=dates.map(day=>slots.map(slot=>`<th class="${day===attendanceViewDate?"selected-day-slot":""}">${escapeHtml(slot)}</th>`).join("")).join("");
  const bodyRows=students.map(student=>`<tr><th class="student-name-cell"><button data-night-student="${escapeHtml(attendanceStudentKey(student))}"><b>${escapeHtml(String(student.number||"").padStart(2,"0"))}번</b><span>${escapeHtml(student.name)}</span></button></th>${dates.map(day=>slots.map(slot=>{const status=resolveStatus(student,day,slot);const programAttrs=status.programId?` data-night-program="${escapeHtml(status.programId)}" data-night-program-kind="${escapeHtml(status.programKind||"general")}"`:"";return `<td class="${day===attendanceViewDate?"selected-day-cell":""}"><button class="night-status-cell ${status.cls}" title="${escapeHtml(status.title)}" data-night-student="${escapeHtml(attendanceStudentKey(student))}" data-night-day="${escapeHtml(day)}" data-night-slot="${escapeHtml(slot)}"${programAttrs}><span>${escapeHtml(status.label)}</span></button></td>`;}).join("")).join("")}</tr>`).join("");
  const matrix=dates.length?`<div class="night-month-scroll-hint">선택일 열은 강조 표시됩니다. 월간 운영일은 항상 유지됩니다. ← 좌우로 스크롤하여 월 전체 확인 →</div><div class="night-month-matrix"><table><thead><tr><th rowspan="2" class="student-name-head">학생</th>${dateHead}</tr><tr>${slotHead}</tr></thead><tbody>${bodyRows}</tbody></table></div>`:`<div class="empty-state compact-empty data-card"><b>${nightStudyOperationStatus(attendanceViewDate).operates?`${nightViewMonth} 야자출결 자료가 없습니다.`:`${attendanceViewDate} · ${escapeHtml(nightStudyOperationStatus(attendanceViewDate).reason)}`}</b><span>${nightStudyOperationStatus(attendanceViewDate).operates?'운영일인데 자료가 없다면 데이터 처리시트의 30_야자출결_정규화를 확인하세요.':'미운영일은 결석·미입력으로 집계하지 않습니다.'}</span></div>`;
  const gradeSummary=classSummaries.reduce((sum,item)=>({roster:sum.roster+item.roster,participants:sum.participants+item.participants,checks:sum.checks+item.checks}),{roster:0,participants:0,checks:0});
  return `<div class="module-page">${tabs}${attendanceDateControls("야자 조회일")}<section class="attendance-dashboard-head night"><div><small>NIGHT STUDY · ${attendanceViewDate} ${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(attendanceViewDate,true))}</small><h3>1학년 선택일 참여현황</h3><p>1학년 전체 재적을 기준으로, 오후자습·야자1·야자2 중 출결 기록이 확인된 학생을 참여로 집계합니다.</p></div><div class="attendance-head-metrics"><span><b>${gradeSummary.roster}</b>1학년 재적인원</span><span><b>${gradeSummary.participants}</b>학년 참여인원</span><span><b>${gradeSummary.checks}</b>선택일 출석체크</span><span><b>${dates.length}</b>${nightViewMonth} 운영일</span></div></section>${classCards}<div class="section-title-row monthly-night-title"><div><small>MONTHLY · ${nightViewMonth}</small><h3>${classNo}반 월간 참여현황</h3></div><span class="state">${attendanceViewDate} ${escapeHtml(weekdayLabelFromKey(attendanceViewDate,true))} 선택</span></div><div class="night-matrix-legend"><span class="present">출석</span><span class="dorm">학사</span><span class="after">방과후 강좌명</span><span class="program">프로그램명</span><span class="late">지각</span><span class="pending">확인</span><span class="none">기록 없음</span></div>${matrix}</div>`;
}

  const students = readonlyCache?.students || [];

  const rows = readonlyCache?.nightAttendance || readonlyCache?.attendance || [];

  $("#drawerTitle")?.classList.add("privacy-sensitive-text");
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="history-summary positive"><span><small>학번</small><b class="privacy-sensitive-text">${escapeHtml(student.studentNo||"-")}</b></span><span><small>학급</small><b>${escapeHtml(student.className||"-")}반</b></span><span><small>일자</small><b>${escapeHtml(day)}</b></span><span><small>시간</small><b>${escapeHtml(slot)}</b></span></div><div class="drawer-section"><h4>야자출결 상세</h4><dl class="detail-list"><dt>결과</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.result||"출석 기록 없음")}</dd><dt>소속·유형</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.affiliation||row?.type||"일반 야자")}</dd><dt>상세</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.detail||"-")}</dd><dt>원본 시간</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.time||row?.period||slot)}</dd></dl></div><p class="drawer-note">방과후·야간심화·추가 프로그램 상태는 해당 프로그램 상세 화면으로 바로 연결됩니다.</p>`;
}

"미신청" : "완료"}</span><span>${i === 2 ? "필요" : "-"}</span><span class="state ${i === 2 ? "warn" : ""}">${i === 2 ? "확인 필요" : "정상"}</span></div>`).join("")}</div></div>`;

function schoolScheduleTimetableMarkup(){
  const anchor=

===== TERM: nightSchedule =====
(no match)

===== TERM: afterSchool =====
--- match 1 at 184099 ---
endance-date-action">${actionHtml}</div>`:""}</div>`;
}
function nightSlotName(rowOrText) {
  const text = typeof rowOrText === "string" ? rowOrText : `${rowOrText?.time || ""} ${rowOrText?.type || ""} ${rowOrText?.detail || ""}`;
  // 운호고 방과후 8교시는 오후자습 시간대이다.
  if (/오후자습|1타임|8교시/.test(text)) return "오후자습";
  if (/야자\s*1|야간\s*1|2타임/.test(text)) return "야자1";
  if (/야자\s*2|야간\s*2|3타임/.test(text)) return "야자2";
  return String(rowOrText?.time || rowOrText?.type || text || "기타").trim();
}
function minutesOfClock(value) {
  const match = String(value || "").match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  return match ? Number(match[1]) * 60 + Number(match[2]) : null;
}
function slotRange(slot) {
  return {"오후자습":[17*60,18*60],"야자1":[19*60+20,20*60+10],"야자2":[20*60+20,21*60+30]}[slot] || null;
}
function isWednesdayKey(value) {
  const key=String(value||"").slice(0,10);
  if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(key)){
    const d=new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
    return !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())&&d.getDay()===3;
  }
  return /^수(?:요일)?$/.test(String(value||"").trim());
}
function programHasNightStudyImpact(program) {
  const dates=[program?.date,...(Array.isArray(program?.dates)?program.dates:[])].filter(Boolean);
  if(dates.length&&dates.every(isWednesdayKey)) return false;
  const weekdayText=String(program?.weekdays||program?.weekday||program?.day||"").replace(/\s/g,"");
  if(weekdayText&&/^(수|수요일)$/.test(weekdayText)) return false;
  // UEP_08177_AFTER_SCHOOL_NIGHT_LINK_ONLY: 문자열 N을 Boolean(N)으로 처리하지 않는다.
  const raw=program?.affectsAttendance??program?.nightLinked??program?.nightLink??program?.['야자연계여부'];
  if(raw===true)return true;
  if(raw===false||raw==null)return false;
  return /^(Y|YES|TRUE|1|예|연계|적용)$/i.test(String(raw).trim());
}
function singlePeriodLabel(session) {
  const candidates=[session?.period,session?.startTime,session?.time].map(v=>String(v||"").trim()).filter(Boolean);
  for(const text of candidates){
    const m=text.match(/(\d+)교시/);
    if(m) return `${m[1]}교시`;
  }
  return String(session?.time||session?.period||"시간 확인");
}
function programOverlapsSlot(program, slot) {
  if(isWednesdayKey(program?.date)||!programHasNightStudyImpact(program)) return false;
  const descriptor = `${program?.time || ""} ${program?.title || ""} ${program?.actualTitle || ""} ${program?.type || ""}`;
  // 차시 시작시각이 8교시라면 시트의 기존 야자영향타임 값보다 학교 운영 기준을 우선한다.
  // 즉 8교시는 야자1이 아니라 오후자습으로만 표시한다.
  if (/8교시/.test(descriptor)) return slot === "오후자습";
  const explicitSlots=(program?.nightSlots||[]).flatMap(value=>{
    const text=String(value||"");
    return ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].filter(name=>text.includes(name));
  });
  if(explicitSlots.length) return explicitSlots.includes(slot);
  const range = slotRange(slot);
  const namedSlot = nightSlotName(descriptor);
  if (namedSlot && ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].includes(namedSlot) && !String(namedSlot).includes("기타")) return namedSlot === slot;
  const clocks = descriptor.match(/\d{1,2}:\d{2}/g) || [];
  if (!range || clocks.length < 2) return Boolean(program?.affectsAttendance && /오후자습/.test(descriptor) && slot === "오후자습");
  const start = minutesOfClock(clocks[0]), end = minutesOfClock(clocks[1]);
  return Number.isFinite(start) && Number.isFinite(end) && start < range[1] && end > range[0];
}
function programParticipationFor(student, day, slot) {
  const id = String(student?.id || student?.studentId || "");
  const number = String(student?.studentNo || "");
  const connected = readonlyCache?.programs || [];
  const derived = typeof afterSchoolProgramGroups === "function" ? afterSchoolProgramGroups() : [];
  const seen = new Set();
  const programs = [...connected, ...derived].filter(program => {
    const key = `${program.id || ""}|${program.date || ""}|${program.title || ""}|${program.time || ""}`;
    if (seen.has(key)) return false; seen.add(key); return true;
  });
  for (const program of programs) {
    if(isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program))continue;
    if (program.date !== day || !programOverlapsSlot(program, slot)) continue;
    const target = (program.students || []).find(item => String(item.studentId || "") === id || (number && String(item.studentNo || item.number || "") === number));
    if (!target) continue;
    const participationText = [target.result,target.attendance,target.participation,target.status,target.checkMethod].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
    const inactive = /결석|불참|취소|중도포기|퇴강|미참여/.test(participationText);
    // 13_출석부의 활성 수강명단은 출석상태가 '등록'이어도 해당 실제 차시에는 프로그램 참여자로 본다.
    // 실제 수업일·야자영향타임은 program.date/program.time 단계에서 이미 검증되므로 명단 등록만으로 다른 날짜에 표시되지 않는다.
    const confirmed = !inactive && ((typeof nightRecordIsAttendance === "function" && nightRecordIsAttendance(target)) || /출석|정상|참여|입실|명단QR|학사|등록|신청/.test(participationText));
    return {program, target, confirmed, pending: !confirmed && !inactive};
  }
  return null;
}
function nightProgramStatus(program){
  const text=`${program?.afterType||""} ${program?.type||""} ${program?.actualTitle||program?.title||""}`;
  if(/야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)) return {cls:"after",label:"야간심화",kind:"after"};
  if(/일반 방과후|방학 방과후|방과후학교/.test(text)) return {cls:"after",label:"방과후",kind:"after"};
  const title=String(program?.actualTitle||program?.title||"프로그램");
  return {cls:"program",label:title.length>8?`${title.slice(0,7)}…`:title,kind:program?.kind||"general"};
}
function attendanceIsWeekday(day){const n=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`).getDay();return n>=1&&n<=5;}
function attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(day){
  const key=String(day||'').slice(0,10);
  const fixed2026=new Set(['2026-01-01','2026-02-16','2026-02-17','2026-02-18','2026-03-01','2026-03-02','2026-05-05','2026-05-24','2026-05-25','2026-06-06','2026-08-15','2026-08-17','2026-09-24','2026-09-25','2026-09-26','2026-10-03','2026-10-05','2026-10-09','2026-12-25']);
  if(fixed2026.has(key))return true;
  const closureRx=/공휴일|대체공휴일|재량휴업|휴업일|개교기념일|임시공휴일|석가탄신일|부처님오신날|어린이날|현충일|광복절|개천절|한글날|성탄절|설날|추석/;
  return schoolEventsForDate(key).some(event=>{
    const title=String(event.title||'').trim(); const type=String(event.type||'').trim();
    if(!closureRx.test(title+' '+type))return false;
    if(/공휴일|대체공휴일|재량휴업|휴업일|개교기념일|임시공휴일/.test(title+' '+type))return true;
    const start=String(event.date||'').slice(0,10), end=String(event.endDate||event.date||'').slice(0,10);
    return start===key&&end===key;
  });
}
function isNightOperatingDay(day){return attendanceIsWeekday(day)&&!attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(day);}
function nearestNightOperatingDay(day){
  let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
  for(let i=0;i<31;i++){const key=dateKey(d);if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;d.setDate(d.getDate()-1);}
  return day;
}
function shiftNightOperatingDate(day,direction){
  let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
  const step=Number(direction)>=0?1:-1;
  for(let i=0;i<62;i++){
    d.setDate(d.getDate()+step);
    const key=dateKey(d);
    if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;
  }
  return day;
}

}
function nightSlotName(rowOrText) {
  const text = typeof rowOrText === "string" ? rowOrText : `${rowOrText?.time || ""} ${rowOrText?.type || ""} ${rowOrText?.detail || ""}`;
  // 운호고 방과후 8교시는 오후자습 시간대이다.
  if (/오후자습|1타임|8교시/.test(text)) return "오후자습";
  if (/야자\s*1|야간\s*1|2타임/.test(text)) return "야자1";
  if (/야자\s*2|야간\s*2|3타임/.test(text)) return "야자2";
  return String(rowOrText?.time || rowOrText?.type || text || "기타").trim();
}
function minutesOfClock(value) {
  const match = String(value || "").match(/(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/);
  return match ? Number(match[1]) * 60 + Number(match[2]) : null;
}
function slotRange(slot) {
  return {"오후자습":[17*60,18*60],"야자1":[19*60+20,20*60+10],"야자2":[20*60+20,21*60+30]}[slot] || null;
}
function isWednesdayKey(value) {
  const key=String(value||"").slice(0,10);
  if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(key)){
    const d=new Date(`${key}T12:00:00`);
    return !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())&&d.getDay()===3;
  }
  return /^수(?:요일)?$/.test(String(value||"").trim());
}
function programHasNightStudyImpact(program) {
  const dates=[program?.date,...(Array.isArray(program?.dates)?program.dates:[])].filter(Boolean);
  if(dates.length&&dates.every(isWednesdayKey)) return false;
  const weekdayText=String(program?.weekdays||program?.weekday||program?.day||"").replace(/\s/g,"");
  if(weekdayText&&/^(수|수요일)$/.test(weekdayText)) return false;
  // UEP_08177_AFTER_SCHOOL_NIGHT_LINK_ONLY: 문자열 N을 Boolean(N)으로 처리하지 않는다.
  const raw=program?.affectsAttendance??program?.nightLinked??program?.nightLink??program?.['야자연계여부'];
  if(raw===true)return true;
  if(raw===false||raw==null)return false;
  return /^(Y|YES|TRUE|1|예|연계|적용)$/i.test(String(raw).trim());
}
function singlePeriodLabel(session) {
  const candidates=[session?.period,session?.startTime,session?.time].map(v=>String(v||"").trim()).filter(Boolean);
  for(const text of candidates){
    const m=text.match(/(\d+)교시/);
    if(m) return `${m[1]}교시`;
  }
  return String(session?.time||session?.period||"시간 확인");
}
function programOverlapsSlot(program, slot) {
  if(isWednesdayKey(program?.date)||!programHasNightStudyImpact(program)) return false;
  const descriptor = `${program?.time || ""} ${program?.title || ""} ${program?.actualTitle || ""} ${program?.type || ""}`;
  // 차시 시작시각이 8교시라면 시트의 기존 야자영향타임 값보다 학교 운영 기준을 우선한다.
  // 즉 8교시는 야자1이 아니라 오후자습으로만 표시한다.
  if (/8교시/.test(descriptor)) return slot === "오후자습";
  const explicitSlots=(program?.nightSlots||[]).flatMap(value=>{
    const text=String(value||"");
    return ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].filter(name=>text.includes(name));
  });
  if(explicitSlots.length) return explicitSlots.includes(slot);
  const range = slotRange(slot);
  const namedSlot = nightSlotName(descriptor);
  if (namedSlot && ["오후자습","야자1","야자2"].includes(namedSlot) && !String(namedSlot).includes("기타")) return namedSlot === slot;
  const clocks = descriptor.match(/\d{1,2}:\d{2}/g) || [];
  if (!range || clocks.length < 2) return Boolean(program?.affectsAttendance && /오후자습/.test(descriptor) && slot === "오후자습");
  const start = minutesOfClock(clocks[0]), end = minutesOfClock(clocks[1]);
  return Number.isFinite(start) && Number.isFinite(end) && start < range[1] && end > range[0];
}
function programParticipationFor(student, day, slot) {
  const id = String(student?.id || student?.studentId || "");
  const number = String(student?.studentNo || "");
  const connected = readonlyCache?.programs || [];
  const derived = typeof afterSchoolProgramGroups === "function" ? afterSchoolProgramGroups() : [];
  const seen = new Set();
  const programs = [...connected, ...derived].filter(program => {
    const key = `${program.id || ""}|${program.date || ""}|${program.title || ""}|${program.time || ""}`;
    if (seen.has(key)) return false; seen.add(key); return true;
  });
  for (const program of programs) {
    if(isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program))continue;
    if (program.date !== day || !programOverlapsSlot(program, slot)) continue;
    const target = (program.students || []).find(item => String(item.studentId || "") === id || (number && String(item.studentNo || item.number || "") === number));
    if (!target) continue;
    const participationText = [target.result,target.attendance,target.participation,target.status,target.checkMethod].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
    const inactive = /결석|불참|취소|중도포기|퇴강|미참여/.test(participationText);
    // 13_출석부의 활성 수강명단은 출석상태가 '등록'이어도 해당 실제 차시에는 프로그램 참여자로 본다.
    // 실제 수업일·야자영향타임은 program.date/program.time 단계에서 이미 검증되므로 명단 등록만으로 다른 날짜에 표시되지 않는다.
    const confirmed = !inactive && ((typeof nightRecordIsAttendance === "function" && nightRecordIsAttendance(target)) || /출석|정상|참여|입실|명단QR|학사|등록|신청/.test(participationText));
    return {program, target, confirmed, pending: !confirmed && !inactive};
  }
  return null;
}
function nightProgramStatus(program){
  const text=`${program?.afterType||""} ${program?.type||""} ${program?.actualTitle||program?.title||""}`;
  if(/야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)) return {cls:"after",label:"야간심화",kind:"after"};
  if(/일반 방과후|방학 방과후|방과후학교/.test(text)) return {cls:"after",label:"방과후",kind:"after"};
  const title=String(program?.actualTitle||program?.title||"프로그램");
  return {cls:"program",label:title.length>8?`${title.slice(0,7)}…`:title,kind:program?.kind||"general"};
}

function attendanceIsSchoolHoliday(day){

  const fixed2026=new Set(['2026-01-01','2026-02-16','2026-02-17','2026-02-18','2026-03-01','2026-03-02','2026-05-05','2026-05-24','2026-05-25','2026-06-06','2026-08-15','2026-08-17','2026-09-24','2026-09-25','2026-09-26','2026-10-03','2026-10-05','2026-10-09','2026-12-25']);

  return schoolEventsForDate(key).some(event=>{
    const title=String(event.title||'').trim(); const type=String(event.type||'').trim();
    if(!closureRx.test(title+' '+type))return false;
    if(/공휴일|대체공휴일|재량휴업|휴업일|개교기념일|임시공휴일/.test(title+' '+type))return true;

    return start===key&&end===key;
  });
}

function nearestNightOperatingDay(day){

  for(let i=0;i<31;i++){const key=dateKey(d);if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;d.setDate(d.getDate()-1);}

function shiftNightOperatingDate(day,direction){
  let d=new Date(`${day}T12:00:00`);
  const step=Number(direction)>=0?1:-1;
  for(let i=0;i<62;i++){
    d.setDate(d.getDate()+step);
    const key=dateKey(d);
    if(isNightOperatingDay(key))return key;
  }
  return day;
}

function inputCenterLibraryRows(){
  const remote=inputCenterHistory().map(x=>({...x,storageMode:x.storageMode||'uep'}));
  // 02 검수·분류와 03 UEP 적용은 [UEP]입력센터 원본을 단일 기준으로 사용합니다.
  // 예전 PC 로컬 보관 이력은 새 정규화 파이프라인에 섞지 않습니다.
  if(inputCenterConfig.libraryScope!=='local') return remote.sort((a,b)=>String(b.createdAt||b.savedAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.createdAt||a.savedAt||'')));
  return (inputCenterLocalHistory||[]).map(x=>({...x,storageMode:'local'})).sort((a,b)=>String(b.createdAt||b.savedAt||'').localeCompare(String(a.createdAt||a.savedAt||'')));
}
function inputCenterProgramSemester(p={}){
  const text=`${p.semester||''} ${p.term||''} ${p.schoolTerm||''} ${p.date||''} ${p.startDate||''}`;
  if(/2학기|(^|[^0-9])2([^0-9]|$)/.test(text)) return '2';
  if(/1학기|(^|[^0-9])1([^0-9]|$)/.test(text)) return '1';
  const m=text.match(/2026[-/.](\d{1,2})/); if(m) return Number(m[1])<=7?'1':'2';
  return '';
}
function inputCenterProgramGroup(p={}){
  const text=`${p.kind||''} ${p.type||''} ${p.afterType||''} ${p.actualTitle||''} ${p.title||''}`;
  if(p.kind==='after'||/방과후|야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)) return /야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)?'night':'after';
  if(p.kind==='common'||/공통/.test(text)) return 'common';
  if(p.kind==='selected'||/선택/.test(text)) return 'selected';
  return '';
}
function inputCenterProgramIdentity(p={}){
  const group=inputCenterProgramGroup(p);
  const title=String(p.actualTitle||p.title||p.name||'').normalize('NFKC').trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ');
  const teacher=String(p.teacher||p.teacherName||p.instructor||'').normalize('NFKC').trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ');
  const semester=inputCenterProgramSemester(p);
  // 방과후/야간심화 readonly 데이터는 차시·출석 결합 과정에서 행별 임시 ID가 달라질 수 있습니다.
  // 연결목록은 강좌 마스터를 선택하는 곳이므로 학기+유형+강좌명+담당교사 기준으로 1회만 노출합니다.
  if(group==='after'||group==='night') return `${group}|${semester}|${title}|${teacher}`;
  const id=String(p.id||p.programId||p.afterId||p.afterSchoolId||p.activityId||p.masterId||'').trim();
  if(id) return `${group}|${id}`;
  return `${group}|${semester}|${title}|${teacher}`;
}
function inputCenterProgramCandidates(purpose=inputCenterConfig.purpose,semester=inputCenterConfig.semester){
  const filtered=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).filter(p=>{
    const group=inputCenterProgramGroup(p); if(!group) return false;
    const sem=inputCenterProgramSemester(p); if(sem&&semester&&sem!==String(semester)) return false;
    if(purpose==='보고서 연결') return (group==='common'||group==='selected') && p.reportRequired!==false && p.reportUse!==false;
    if(purpose==='프로그램 참가신청') return group==='selected'||group==='after'||group==='night';
    if(purpose==='만족도조사') return group==='common'||group==='selected'||group==='after'||group==='night';
    return false;
  });
  // readonly 프로그램 데이터에는 방과후 차시/일정 결합 때문에 같은 마스터 강좌가 여러 행으로 들어올 수 있습니다.
  // 입력센터 연결목록은 차시가 아니라 '프로그램 마스터 1건'을 선택하는 곳이므로 안정 ID 기준으로 1회만 노출합니다.
  const deduped=[]; const seen=new Set();
  filtered.forEach(p=>{const key=inputCenterProgramIdentity(p);if(seen.has(key))return;seen.add(key);deduped.push(p);});
  const rank={common:1,selected:2,after:3,night:4};
  return deduped.sort((a,b)=>(rank[inputCenterProgramGroup(a)]||9)-(rank[inputCenterProgramGroup(b)]||9)||String(a.actualTitle||a.title||'').localeCompare(String(b.actualTitle||b.title||'')));
}
function inputCenterProgramLabel(p={}){
  const g=inputCenterProgramGroup(p); const prefix={common:'공통',selected:'선택',after:'방과후',night:'야간심화'}[g]||'프로그램';
  const title=p.actualTitle||p.title||p.name||'';
  return `${prefix} · ${title}`;
}

function reportCategoryKey(row){
  const text=`${row?.kind||''} ${row?.type||''} ${row?.area||''} ${row?.reportGroup||''} ${row?.programName||''}`;
  const common=/공통/.test(text)||String(row?.kind||'').includes('common');
  const career=/진로/.test(text);
  return `${common?'common':'selected'}-${career?'career':'autonomy'}`;
}
function programCategoryKey(p){
  if(p?.kind==='after'||/방과후|야간심화/.test(`${p?.kind||''} ${p?.type||''} ${p?.title||''}`))return 'after';
  const common=p?.kind==='common'||/공통/.test(`${p?.kind||''} ${p?.type||''}`);
  const career=/진로/.test(`${p?.type||''} ${p?.area||''} ${p?.recordTitle||''}`);
  return `${common?'common':'selected'}-${career?'career':'autonomy'}`;
}

function afterSchoolProgramGroups() {
  const connected = (readonlyCache?.programs || []).map(programWithOverride).filter((program) => program.kind === "after").map((program) => ({...program, reportRequired:false}));
  const rows = (readonlyCache?.afterSchoolAttendance || []).slice();
  const groups = new Map();
  rows.forEach((row) => {
    const type = String(row.type || "방과후 프로그램").trim() || "방과후 프로그램";
    const time = String(row.time || row.period || "").trim();
    const key = [row.date || "", type, time].join("|");
    if (!groups.has(key)) groups.set(key, { key, id: `after-${encodeURIComponent(key)}`, date: row.date || "", title: type, actualTitle: type, recordTitle: type, time: time || "시간 미등록", place: row.detail || "운영정보 확인", teacher: "", records: [], kind: "after", reportRequired: false, affectsAttendance: /오후자습/.test(type), source:"30_야자출결_정규화" });
    groups.get(key).records.push(row);
  });
  const derived = [...groups.values()].map((program) => {
    const seen = new Set();
    program.students = program.records.filter((row) => {
      const key = attendanceStudentKey(row);
      if (seen.has(key)) return false;

function afterSchoolProgramGroups() {
  const connected = (readonlyCache?.programs || []).map(programWithOverride).filter((program) => program.kind === "after").map((program) => ({...program, reportRequired:false}));
  const rows = (readonlyCache?.afterSchoolAttendance || []).slice();
  const groups = new Map();
  rows.forEach((row) => {
    const type = String(row.type || "방과후 프로그램").trim() || "방과후 프로그램";
    const time = String(row.time || row.period || "").trim();
    const key = [row.date || "", type, time].join("|");
    if (!groups.has(key)) groups.set(key, { key, id: `after-${encodeURIComponent(key)}`, date: row.date || "", title: type, actualTitle: type, recordTitle: type, time: time || "시간 미등록", place: row.detail || "운영정보 확인", teacher: "", records: [], kind: "after", reportRequired: false, affectsAttendance: /오후자습/.test(type), source:"30_야자출결_정규화" });
    groups.get(key).records.push(row);
  });
  const derived = [...groups.values()].map((program) => {
    const seen = new Set();
    program.students = program.records.filter((row) => {
      const key = attendanceStudentKey(row);
      if (seen.has(key)) return false;
      seen.add(key);
      return true;
    });
    program.attendedCount = program.students.filter(nightRecordIsAttendance).length;
    return program;
  });
  const connectedKeys = new Set(connected.map((program)=>`${program.date}|${program.title}|${program.time}`));
  return [...connected, ...derived.filter((program)=>!connectedKeys.has(`${program.date}|${program.title}|${program.time}`))].sort((a,b) => String(b.date).localeCompare(String(a.date)) || String(a.title).localeCompare(String(b.title)));
}

function programWithOverride(program) {
  if (!program || typeof program !== "object") return program;
  const override = state.programOverrides?.[program.id] || {};
  const merged = {...program, ...override, students: program.students || []};
  // 보고서 폼 URL은 프로그램별 로컬 override가 아니라 00_UEP설정의 공통값이 원본입니다.
  // 과거 로컬 빈값/구버전 override가 공통 URL을 가리지 않도록 화면 직전에도 표준 설정값을 우선 적용합니다.
  const uepSettings = readonlyCache?.uepSettings || {};
  if (program.kind === "selected") merged.reportFormUrl = String(uepSettings.SELECTED_ACTIVITY_REPORT_FORM_URL || program.reportFormUrl || "").trim();
  if (program.kind === "common") merged.reportFormUrl = String(uepSettings.COMMON_ACTIVITY_REPORT_FORM_URL || program.reportFormUrl || "").trim();
  return merged;
}
function programAreaKind(program) {
  if (program?.kind === "after" || /방과후/.test(String(program?.type || ""))) return "after";
  const text = `${program?.type || ""} ${program?.recordTitle || ""} ${program?.actualTitle || program?.title || ""}`;
  if (/진로/.test(text)) return "career";
  return "autonomy";
}
function programAreaLabel(program) {
  const kind = programAreaKind(program);
  if (kind === "career") return "진로";
  if (kind === "after") {
    const text = `${program?.afterType || ""} ${program?.type || ""} ${program?.actualTitle || program?.title || ""}`;
    if (/야간심화|심화수업/.test(text)) return "야간심화수업";
    return "방과후";
  }
  return "자율";
}

function programsView() {
  const monthLabel=`${programMonth.slice(0,4)}년 ${Number(programMonth.slice(5,7))}월`;
  const connectedPrograms = readonlyCache?.programs || [];
  const localSelected=(state.programs||[]).filter(program=>program.kind!=="after").map(program=>({...program,kind:"selected",actualTitle:program.actualTitle||program.title,recordTitle:program.recordTitle||program.title,reportRequired:program.reportRequired!==false,attendedCount:Array.isArray(program.students)?program.students.length:0,source:"이 PC 로컬"}));
  const localAfter=(state.programs||[]).filter(program=>program.kind==="after").map(program=>({...program,kind:"after",actualTitle:program.actualTitle||program.title,recordTitle:"생활기록부 기록 대상 아님",reportRequired:false,reportToAuthority:false,attendedCount:Array.isArray(program.students)?program.students.length:0,source:"이 PC 로컬",affectsAttendance:true}));
  const commonItems=connectedPrograms.filter(program=>program.kind==="common");
  const selectedConnected=connectedPrograms.filter(program=>program.kind==="selected" || program.kind==="general");

let googleConnectionStatus = { ok: false, encryption: false };
let readonlyCache = null;
let googleConnectionError = "";
let uepSchoolReadDiagnostics = [];
function uepDiagSafe(value,depth=0){
  if(depth>6)return '[DEPTH_LIMIT]';
  if(value==null||typeof value==='number'||typeof value==='boolean')return value;
  if(typeof value==='string')return value.replace(/[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}/gi,'[EMAIL_REDACTED]').slice(0,4000);
  if(Array.isArray(value))return value.slice(0,30).map(v=>uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1));
  if(typeof value==='object'){
    const out={};
    for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(value)){
      if(/token|secret|password|authorization|cookie|credential|private|email|name/i.test(k)){out[k]='[REDACTED]';continue;}
      out[k]=uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1);
    }
    return out;
  }
  return String(value);
}
function uepDiagRecord(stage,value){
  uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.push({at:new Date().toISOString(),stage,value:uepDiagSafe(value)});
  if(uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.length>80)uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.shift();
}
async function downloadSchoolReadDiagnostics(){
  let verified=null;
  try{verified=await window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus?.({verify:true});uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify',verified);}
  catch(error){uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});}
  const report={
    schema:'uep-school-read-diagnostic-v1',
    appVersion:APP_VERSION,
    exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
    platform:navigator.platform,
    online:navigator.onLine,
    schoolReadBridge:{
      sessionStatus:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus),
      login:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadLogin),
      preview:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync),
      readCache:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache)
    },
    googleConnectionStatus:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionStatus),
    googleConnectionError:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionError),
    cacheSummary:{
      syncedAt:readonlyCache?.syncedAt||null,
      students:readonlyCache?.students?.length||0,
      dormStudents:readonlyCache?.dormStudents?.length||0
    },
    verifiedSession:uepDiagSafe(verified),
    events:uepSchoolReadDiagnostics
  };
  const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(report,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=url;
  a.download='UEP-school-read-diagnostic-'+new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g,'-')+'.json';
  document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),2000);
  toast('진단 JSON 파일을 저장했습니다. 이 파일을 채팅에 올려 주세요.');
}

  }
  return String(value);
}
function uepDiagRecord(stage,value){
  uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.push({at:new Date().toISOString(),stage,value:uepDiagSafe(value)});
  if(uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.length>80)uepSchoolReadDiagnostics.shift();
}
async function downloadSchoolReadDiagnostics(){
  let verified=null;
  try{verified=await window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus?.({verify:true});uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify',verified);}
  catch(error){uepDiagRecord('export.sessionVerify.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});}
  const report={
    schema:'uep-school-read-diagnostic-v1',
    appVersion:APP_VERSION,
    exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
    platform:navigator.platform,
    online:navigator.onLine,
    schoolReadBridge:{
      sessionStatus:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus),
      login:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadLogin),
      preview:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.previewReadonlySync),
      readCache:Boolean(window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache)
    },
    googleConnectionStatus:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionStatus),
    googleConnectionError:uepDiagSafe(googleConnectionError),
    cacheSummary:{
      syncedAt:readonlyCache?.syncedAt||null,
      students:readonlyCache?.students?.length||0,
      dormStudents:readonlyCache?.dormStudents?.length||0
    },
    verifiedSession:uepDiagSafe(verified),
    events:uepSchoolReadDiagnostics
  };
  const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(report,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
  const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a=document.createElement('a');
  a.href=url;
  a.download='UEP-school-read-diagnostic-'+new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g,'-')+'.json';
  document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),2000);
  toast('진단 JSON 파일을 저장했습니다. 이 파일을 채팅에 올려 주세요.');
}

      : JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("gyomuon-state") || "null");

  } catch (error) {
    console.warn("[UEP] 로컬 상태 불러오기 실패", error);

    const authSnapshot=state?.auth ? structuredClone(state.auth) : structuredClone(baseState.auth);
    const settingsSnapshot=state?.settings ? structuredClone(state.settings) : structuredClone(baseState.settings);
    state=structuredClone(baseState);
    state.auth={...state.auth,...authSnapshot};
    state.settings={...state.settings,...settingsSnapshot};
    try { normalizeSavedState(); } catch (_) {}
  }
  // 자동로그인을 선택한 사용자는 네트워크/Google Sheet 확인을 기다리지 않고
  // 마지막 정상 로컬 사용자 세션으로 즉시 UI를 연다. 계정 유효성은 백그라운드 동기화 후 재검증한다.
  let resumedRememberedSession=false;
  try {
    resumedRememberedSession=await withStartupTimeout(restoreRememberedSessionImmediately(),1200,false);
  } catch (rememberError) {
    console.warn('[UEP] 자동 사용자 복원 실패 - 화면을 먼저 엽니다.', rememberError);
    resumedRememberedSession=false;
  }
  // 과거 개발용 샘플은 실제 학교 자료처럼 남지 않도록 자동 정리한다.
  state.programs = (state.programs || []).filter(program => String(program?.title || "").trim() !== "진로 탐색 특강");
  const allowedPages = new Set(["dashboard","students","dorm","timetable","attendance","scores","admissions","records","programs","alerts","calendar","work","duties","forms","outputs","settings","help"]);
  if (!allowedPages.has(state.activePage)) state.activePage = "dashboard";
  try { navigate(state.activePage || "dashboard"); } catch (startupRenderError) { console.warn("[UEP] 초기 화면 복구", startupRenderError); state.activePage="dashboard"; try { navigate("dashboard"); } catch (dashboardRenderError) { console.error("[UEP] 대시보드 초기 렌더 실패", dashboardRenderError); } }
  refreshHeaderRoleBadge();
  try {
    const bootContent=document.getElementById('pageContent');
    if(bootContent && !String(bootContent.innerHTML||'').trim()){
      state.activePage='dashboard';
      navigate('dashboard');
    }
  } catch (bootRenderError) {
    console.error('[UEP] 부팅 대시보드 강제 복구 실패',bootRenderError);
  }

  // 외부 연결은 UI 초기화와 분리하고 서로 독립적인 작업은 병렬로 시작한다.
  // NEIS가 Google 캐시 완료를 기다리지 않도록 먼저 예약한다.
  setTimeout(() => loadNeisDashboard(false), 0);
async function ensureSchoolReadSessionForCurrentUser({verify=true}={}){
  uepDiagRecord('session.ensure.start',{verify,online:navigator.onLine});
  if(!window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus){
    const missing={authenticated:false,reason:'School Read API 세션 기능 없음'};

  catch(error){uepDiagRecord('session.status.before.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});}
  if(status?.authenticated)return status;
  const saved=state?.auth?.user||state?.auth?.rememberedUser||state?.settings?.userProfile||{};
  const name=String(saved?.name||state?.settings?.loginUserName||state?.settings?.teacherName||'').trim();
  const email=String(saved?.email||'').trim();
  uepDiagRecord('session.identity.available',{hasName:Boolean(name),hasEmail:Boolean(email),source:state?.auth?.user?'auth.user':state?.auth?.rememberedUser?'auth.rememberedUser':'settings.userProfile'});
  if(!name||!email||!window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadLogin){
    const missing={authenticated:false,reason:!name||!email?'저장된 담임 이름·이메일 없음':'School Read 로그인 기능 없음'};
    uepDiagRecord('session.login.skipped',missing);return status||missing;
  }
  let login=null;
  try{login=await window.schoolBoard.schoolReadLogin({name,email});uepDiagRecord('session.login.result',login);}
  catch(error){uepDiagRecord('session.login.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});return {authenticated:false,reason:error?.message||'학교 공용 읽기 로그인 예외'};}
  if(!login?.ok)return {authenticated:false,reason:login?.message||login?.reason||'학교 공용 읽기 로그인 실패'};
  try{status=await window.schoolBoard.schoolReadSessionStatus({verify:true});uepDiagRecord('session.status.after',status);}
  catch(error){uepDiagRecord('session.status.after.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});return {authenticated:false,reason:error?.message||'로그인 후 세션 검증 예외'};}

    : Promise.resolve();

    ? withStartupTimeout(window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache(), 8000, null)

        })
    : Promise.resolve();
  await Promise.all([credentialTask, cacheTask]);
  // 화면은 로컬/메모리 캐시로 먼저 표시한 뒤, 실행당 한 번만 백그라운드 원격 동기화한다.

function reportCategoryKey(row){
  const text=`${row?.kind||''} ${row?.type||''} ${row?.area||''} ${row?.reportGroup||''} ${row?.programName||''}`;
  const common=/공통/.test(text)||String(row?.kind||'').includes('common');
  const career=/진로/.test(text);
  return `${common?'common':'selected'}-${career?'career':'autonomy'}`;
}
function programCategoryKey(p){
  if(p?.kind==='after'||/방과후|야간심화/.test(`${p?.kind||''} ${p?.type||''} ${p?.title||''}`))return 'after';
  const common=p?.kind==='common'||/공통/.test(`${p?.kind||''} ${p?.type||''}`);
  const career=/진로/.test(`${p?.type||''} ${p?.area||''} ${p?.recordTitle||''}`);
  return `${common?'common':'selected'}-${career?'career':'autonomy'}`;
}

function outputAfterCourseKey(program){return String(program?.courseId||program?.programId||program?.id||'').replace(/^after-(?:course|master|session)-/,'');}
function afterschoolIntegratedAttendancePreview(course){
  const all=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).filter(p=>p?.kind==='after'&&!p.isCourseMaster&&!/일정확인필요|취소|휴강|폐강/.test(String(p.status||p.operationStatus||'')));
  const key=outputAfterCourseKey(course);
  const sessions=all.filter(s=>outputAfterCourseKey(s)===key).sort((a,b)=>String(a.date||'').localeCompare(String(b.date||''))||Number(a.sessionNo||0)-Number(b.sessionNo||0));
  const rosterMap=new Map();
  [course,...sessions].forEach(p=>(p.students||[]).forEach(st=>{const k=String(st.studentId||st.studentNo||st.number||st.name||'');if(k&&!rosterMap.has(k))rosterMap.set(k,st);}));
  const roster=[...rosterMap.values()].sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo||a.number||'').localeCompare(String(b.studentNo||b.number||'')));
  const head=sessions.map((session,i)=>`<th><b>${escapeHtml(session.sessionNo||i+1)}차시</b><small>${escapeHtml(String(session.date||'').slice(5).replace('-','/'))}</small></th>`).join('');
  const rows=roster.map((student,i)=>`<tr><td>${i+1}</td><td>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo||student.number||'-')}</td><td><b>${escapeHtml(student.name||'-')}</b></td>${sessions.map(session=>`<td class="attendance-mark">${escapeHtml(resolvedAfterAttendanceStatus(session,student)||'-')}</td>`).join('')}<td></td></tr>`).join('');
  return `<div class="output-attendance-summary"><span><small>강좌</small><b>${escapeHtml(course.actualTitle||course.title||'-')}</b></span><span><small>수강학생</small><b>${roster.length}명</b></span><span><small>전체 차시</small><b>${sessions.length}차시</b></span><span><small>담당</small><b>${escapeHtml(course.teacher||sessions[0]?.teacher||'-')}</b></span></div><div class="output-attendance-scroll"><table class="output-attendance-table"><thead><tr><th>순번</th><th>학번</th><th>이름</th>${head}<th>비고</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows||'<tr><td colspan="99">출석부 학생 자료가 없습니다.</td></tr>'}</tbody></table></div>`;
}

  const all=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).filter(p=>p?.kind==='after'&&!p.isCourseMaster&&!/일정확인필요|취소|휴강|폐강/.test(String(p.status||p.operationStatus||'')));
  const key=outputAfterCourseKey(course);
  const sessions=all.filter(s=>outputAfterCourseKey(s)===key).sort((a,b)=>String(a.date||'').localeCompare(String(b.date||''))||Number(a.sessionNo||0)-Number(b.sessionNo||0));
  const rosterMap=new Map();
  [course,...sessions].forEach(p=>(p.students||[]).forEach(st=>{const k=String(st.studentId||st.studentNo||st.number||st.name||'');if(k&&!rosterMap.has(k))rosterMap.set(k,st);}));
  const roster=[...rosterMap.values()].sort((a,b)=>String(a.studentNo||a.number||'').localeCompare(String(b.studentNo||b.number||'')));
  const head=sessions.map((session,i)=>`<th><b>${escapeHtml(session.sessionNo||i+1)}차시</b><small>${escapeHtml(String(session.date||'').slice(5).replace('-','/'))}</small></th>`).join('');
  const rows=roster.map((student,i)=>`<tr><td>${i+1}</td><td>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo||student.number||'-')}</td><td><b>${escapeHtml(student.name||'-')}</b></td>${sessions.map(session=>`<td class="attendance-mark">${escapeHtml(resolvedAfterAttendanceStatus(session,student)||'-')}</td>`).join('')}<td></td></tr>`).join('');
  return `<div class="output-attendance-summary"><span><small>강좌</small><b>${escapeHtml(course.actualTitle||course.title||'-')}</b></span><span><small>수강학생</small><b>${roster.length}명</b></span><span><small>전체 차시</small><b>${sessions.length}차시</b></span><span><small>담당</small><b>${escapeHtml(course.teacher||sessions[0]?.teacher||'-')}</b></span></div><div class="output-attendance-scroll"><table class="output-attendance-table"><thead><tr><th>순번</th><th>학번</th><th>이름</th>${head}<th>비고</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows||'<tr><td colspan="99">출석부 학생 자료가 없습니다.</td></tr>'}</tbody></table></div>`;
}

function normalizeSavedState() {
  const validPages = new Set(["dashboard","students","dorm","timetable","attendance","scores","admissions","records","programs","alerts","calendar","work","duties","forms","outputs","settings","help"]);
  if (!validPages.has(state.activePage)) state.activePage = "dashboard";
  if (!["mine","homeroom","student","teacher"].includes(state.timetableTab)) state.timetableTab = "mine";
  if (!["month","week"].includes(state.calendarMode)) state.calendarMode = "month";

  state.tasks = Array.isArray(state.tasks) ? state.tasks.filter(Boolean).map(task=>({status:task.done?"done":(task.status||"planned"),...task})) : structuredClone(baseState.tasks);
  ensureDevelopmentTasks();
  state.programEvidence = state.programEvidence && typeof state.programEvidence === "object" ? state.programEvidence : {};
  state.issueReports = Array.isArray(state.issueReports) ? state.issueReports.filter(Boolean) : [];
  state.programAdminReports = state.programAdminReports && typeof state.programAdminReports === "object" ? state.programAdminReports : {};
  state.programOverrides = state.programOverrides && typeof state.programOverrides === "object" ? state.programOverrides : {};
  state.autoAlertAcks = state.autoAlertAcks && typeof state.autoAlertAcks === "object" ? state.autoAlertAcks : {};
  state.programs = (Array.isArray(state.programs) ? state.programs : structuredClone(baseState.programs))
    .filter((program) => program && typeof program === "object")
    .map((program) => ({
      ...program,
      students: (Array.isArray(program.students) ? program.students : []).map((student) => {
        if (student && typeof student === "object")
          return { number: String(student.number || ""), name: String(student.name || "") };
        const [number = "", ...name] = String(student || "").trim().split(/\s+/);
        return { number, name: name.join(" ") };
      }),
    }));

  state.gradeNotices = (Array.isArray(state.gradeNotices) ? state.gradeNotices : structuredClone(baseState.gradeNotices))
    .filter((item) => item && typeof item === "object" && String(item.title || "").trim() !== "방학식 학생 안내 확인")
    .map((item) => ({
      ...item,
      requiredClasses: Array.isArray(item.requiredClasses) ? item.requiredClasses : [],
      confirmedClasses: Array.isArray(item.confirmedClasses) ? item.confirmedClasses : [],
    }));
  state.reportDeadlines = (Array.isArray(state.reportDeadlines) ? state.reportDeadlines : structuredClone(baseState.reportDeadlines)).filter(Boolean);
  state.schoolDeadlines = (Array.isArray(state.schoolDeadlines) ? state.schoolDeadlines : structuredClone(baseState.schoolDeadlines)).filter(Boolean);
  state.dormOutings = (Array.isArray(state.dormOutings) ? state.dormOutings : structuredClone(baseState.dormOutings))
    .filter((item) => item && typeof item === "object")
    .map((item) => ({ date: dateKey(today), ...item }));

  if (!state.settings || typeof state.settings !== "object") state.settings = structuredClone(baseState.settings);
  state.settings.links = { ...baseState.settings.links, ...(state.settings.links && typeof state.settings.links === "object" ? state.settings.links : {}) };
  const canonicalLinks={portal:"https://cbe.eduptl.kr/bpm_lgn_lg00_001.do",edufine:"https://klef.cbe.go.kr/keris_ui/main.do",riro:"https://unho.riroschool.kr/",unhoLlm:"https://unho.kr/"}; Object.entries(canonicalLinks).forEach

    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{const target=$('#scoreStudent');if(target){target.value=student.id;$('#scoreQueryButton')?.click();}});
  }else if(page==='admissions'){
    const scope=$('#admissionScope'),cls=$('#admissionClass'); if(scope)scope.value='individual';
    if(cls){cls.value=classNo;cls.dispatchEvent(new Event('change'));}
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{const target=$('#admissionStudent');if(target){target.value=student.id;$('#admissionQueryButton')?.click();}});
  }else if(page==='attendance'){
    attendanceStatStudentFilter=attendanceStudentKey(student);
  }else if(page==='records'){
    recordQueryMode='student'; recordClassNo=classNo||recordClassNo; recordStudentId=student.id;
  }
}
function goFromStudentDashboard(page,target=''){
  const student=studentFocusStudent();
  if(page==='scores')scoreMode='combined';
  if(page==='admissions')admissionMode=target==='combined'?'combined':(target||'combined');
  if(page==='attendance')attendanceMode=['official','late','night'].includes(target)?target:'statistics';
  closeDrawer();
  navigate(page);
  // navigate()는 좌측 메뉴 진입 규칙에 따라 생활기록부 첫 탭을 활동내역으로 초기화한다.
  // 학생 대시보드의 선택과목 전체보기에서는 해당 학생·교육과정 탭을 다시 명시하여 흐름을 보존한다.
  if(page==='records'){
    recordMode=target==='curriculum'?'curriculum':target==='final'?'final':'activities';
    recordQueryMode='student';
    if(student){recordStudentId=student.id;recordClassNo=recordStudentClass(student)||recordClassNo;}
    render('records');
  }
  if(page==='attendance'){
    attendanceMode=['official','late','night'].includes(target)?target:'statistics';
    if(student)attendanceStatStudentFilter=attendanceStudentKey(student);
    render('attendance');
    window.scrollTo(0,0);
  }
}

function navigate(page) {
  if(setupWizardActive){ renderSetupWizard(); return; }
  // v0.78.7: hidden legacy pages are routed into their integrated hubs.
  const routedTimetable=page==="timetable", routedDuties=page==="duties", routedForms=page==="forms";
  if(routedTimetable){ calendarHubMode="timetable"; page="calendar"; }
  if(routedDuties){ workEmbeddedMode="duties"; page="work"; }
  if(routedForms){ workEmbeddedMode="forms"; page="work"; }
  if(page==="alerts"){ outputHubMode="alerts"; page="outputs"; }
  if(page==="calendar" && state.activePage!=="calendar" && !routedTimetable) calendarHubMode="schedule";
  if(page==="work" && state.activePage!=="work" && !routedDuties && !routedForms) workEmbeddedMode="home";
  // 좌측 메뉴를 다시 누르거나 다른 메뉴로 이동하면 해당 모듈의 첫 화면부터 시작합니다.
  if(page==="inputs"){
    inputCenterConfig.manualText="";
  }else if(page==="outputs"){
    outputCenterState.home=true;
    outputCenterState.selected.clear();
    outputCenterState.reportScope="program";
  }else if(page==="records"){

function openNightAttendanceDetail(studentKey, day, slot) {
  const students = readonlyCache?.students || [];
  const student = students.find(item => attendanceStudentKey(item) === studentKey) || {};
  const rows = readonlyCache?.nightAttendance || readonlyCache?.attendance || [];

  openDrawer(`${student.name || "학생"} · ${day} ${slot}`);

  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="history-summary positive"><span><small>학번</small><b class="privacy-sensitive-text">${escapeHtml(student.studentNo||"-")}</b></span><span><small>학급</small><b>${escapeHtml(student.className||"-")}반</b></span><span><small>일자</small><b>${escapeHtml(day)}</b></span><span><small>시간</small><b>${escapeHtml(slot)}</b></span></div><div class="drawer-section"><h4>야자출결 상세</h4><dl class="detail-list"><dt>결과</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.result||"출석 기록 없음")}</dd><dt>소속·유형</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.affiliation||row?.type||"일반 야자")}</dd><dt>상세</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.detail||"-")}</dd><dt>원본 시간</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.time||row?.period||slot)}</dd></dl></div><p class="drawer-note">방과후·야간심화·추가 프로그램 상태는 해당 프로그램 상세 화면으로 바로 연결됩니다.</p>`;
}

function schoolScheduleTimetableMarkup(){
  const anchor=new Date(`${calendarSelectedDate||dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
  const monday=new Date(anchor); monday.setDate(anchor.getDate()-((anchor.getDay()+6)%7));
  const days=Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>{const d=new Date(monday);d.setDate(monday.getDate()+i);return d;});
  const dayEvents=days.map(d=>schoolEventsForDate(dateKey(d)).filter(calendarEventMatchesFilter).sort((a,b)=>String(a.time||"99:99").localeCompare(String(b.time||"99:99"))));
  const maxRows=Math.max(1,...dayEvents.map(rows=>rows.length));
  const rows=Array.from({length:maxRows},(_,rowIndex)=>`<div class="school-schedule-row"><div class="school-schedule-slot">${rowIndex+1}</div>${dayEvents.map(events=>{const event=events[rowIndex];if(!event)return '<div class="school-schedule-cell empty"><span>—</span></div>';const kind=calendarEventVisualKind(event);return `<button class="school-schedule-cell kind-${kind} ${calendarEventIsPending(event)?"pending":""}" data-school-event="${escapeHtml(event.id)}"><time>${escapeHtml(event.time||"종일")}</time><b>${escapeHtml(calendarShortTitle(calendarDisplayTitle(event),26))}</b><small>${escapeHtml([event.target,event.place].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")||event.owner||"학교일정")}</small></button>`;}).join("")}</div>`).join("");
  return `<section class="school-schedule-timetable"><div class="school-schedule-head"><div class="school-schedule-corner">순서</div>${days.map(d=>`<button data-calendar-date="${dateKey(d)}" class="${dateKey(d)===dateKey(today)?"today":""}"><b>${["일","월","화","수","목","금","토"][d.getDay()]}</b><span>${d.getMonth()+1}/${d.getDate()}</span><em>${schoolEventsForDate(dateKey(d)).length}건</em></button>`).join("")}</div>${rows}</section>`;
}
function calendarView() {
  const hubTabs=`<div class="uep-hub-tabs calendar-hub-tabs"><button class="${calendarHubMode==="schedule"?"active":""}" data-calendar-hub="schedule"><b>학교일정</b><small>월간·주간 일정</small></button><button class="${calendarHubMode==="timetable"?"active":""}" data-calendar-hub="timetable"><b>시간표</b><small>개인·학급·학생·교사</small></button></div>`;
  if(calendarHubMode==="timetable") return `<div class="module-page calendar-timetable-hub">${hubTabs}${timetableView()}</div>`;
  const anchor=new Date(`${calendarSelectedDate||dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
  return `<div class="module-page calendar-page-v654 school-schedule-page">${hubTabs}<div class="calendar-page-toolbar"><div><small>SCHOOL SCHEDULE · 데이터처리시트 41_학교캘린더</small><h3>학교일정</h3><p>월간계획을 기본으로 확인하고 월간·주간 보기를 자유롭게 전환합니다.</p></div><div><button class="btn secondary" data-calendar-today>${state.calendarMode==="week"?"이번 주":"오늘"}</button><button class="btn primary" data-reference="calendar-add">＋ 개인 일정</button></div></div><div class="school-schedule-summary"><b>${anchor.getFullYear()}년 ${anchor.getMonth()+1}월</b><span>선택일 ${anchor.getMonth()+1}/${anchor.getDate()} · ${schoolEventsForDate(dateKey(anchor)).length}건</span></div><div class="card calendar-page-card school-schedule-card">${calendarMarkup(true)}<div class="widget-source-footer dashboard-source">출처: 데이터처리시트 41_학교캘린더 · 대시보드 월간/주간과 동일 DB</div></div></div>`;
}

function openNightAttendanceDetail(studentKey, day, slot) {
  const students = readonlyCache?.students || [];
  const student = students.find(item => attendanceStudentKey(item) === studentKey) || {};
  const rows = readonlyCache?.nightAttendance || readonlyCache?.attendance || [];
  const row = rows.find(item => item.date === day && nightSlotName(item) === slot && (attendanceStudentKey(item) === studentKey || String(item.studentNo||"") === String(student.studentNo||"")));
  openDrawer(`${student.name || "학생"} · ${day} ${slot}`);
  $("#drawerTitle")?.classList.add("privacy-sensitive-text");
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="history-summary positive"><span><small>학번</small><b class="privacy-sensitive-text">${escapeHtml(student.studentNo||"-")}</b></span><span><small>학급</small><b>${escapeHtml(student.className||"-")}반</b></span><span><small>일자</small><b>${escapeHtml(day)}</b></span><span><small>시간</small><b>${escapeHtml(slot)}</b></span></div><div class="drawer-section"><h4>야자출결 상세</h4><dl class="detail-list"><dt>결과</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.result||"출석 기록 없음")}</dd><dt>소속·유형</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.affiliation||row?.type||"일반 야자")}</dd><dt>상세</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.detail||"-")}</dd><dt>원본 시간</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.time||row?.period||slot)}</dd></dl></div><p class="drawer-note">방과후·야간심화·추가 프로그램 상태는 해당 프로그램 상세 화면으로 바로 연결됩니다.</p>`;
}

function subjectsView() {
  return `<div class="module-page">${toolbar("학생·희망과목·오류 검색", "신청 입력")}<div class="data-card"><div class="data-head student-grid"><span>학번</span><span>학생</span><span>1차 신청</span><span>상담</span><span>상태</span></div>${samples.students.map((r, i) => `<div class="data-row student-grid"><b>${r[0]}</b><span>${r[1]}</span><span>${i === 2 ? "미신청" : "완료"}</span><span>${i === 2 ? "필요" : "-"}</span><span class="state ${i === 2 ? "warn" : ""}">${i === 2 ? "확인 필요" : "정상"}</span></div>`).join("")}</div></div>`;
}
function schoolScheduleTimetableMarkup(){
  const anchor=new Date(`${calendarSelectedDate||dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
  const monday=new Date(anchor); monday.setDate(anchor.getDate()-((anchor.getDay()+6)%7));
  const days=Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>{const d=new Date(monday);d.setDate(monday.getDate()+i);return d;});
  const dayEvents=days.map(d=>schoolEventsForDate(dateKey(d)).filter(calendarEventMatchesFilter).sort((a,b)=>String(a.time||"99:99").localeCompare(String(b.time||"99:99"))));
  const maxRows=Math.max(1,...dayEvents.map(rows=>rows.length));
  const rows=Array.from({length:maxRows},(_,rowIndex)=>`<div class="school-schedule-row"><div class="school-schedule-slot">${rowIndex+1}</div>${dayEvents.map(events=>{const event=events[rowIndex];if(!event)return '<div class="school-schedule-cell empty"><span>—</span></div>';const kind=calendarEventVisualKind(event);return `<button class="school-schedule-cell kind-${kind} ${calendarEventIsPending(event)?"pending":""}" data-school-event="${escapeHtml(event.id)}"><time>${escapeHtml(event.time||"종일")}</time><b>${escapeHtml(calendarShortTitle(calendarDisplayTitle(event),26))}</b><small>${escapeHtml([event.target,event.place].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")||event.owner||"학교일정")}</small></button>`;}).join("")}</div>`).join("");
  return `<section class="school-schedule-timetable"><div class="school-schedule-head"><div class="school-schedule-corner">순서</div>${days.map(d=>`<button data-calendar-date="${dateKey(d)}" class="${dateKey(d)===dateKey(today)?"today":""}"><b>${["일","월","화","수","목","금","토"][d.getDay()]}</b><span>${d.getMonth()+1}/${d.getDate()}</span><em>${schoolEventsForDate(dateKey(d)).length}건</em></button>`).join("")}</div>${rows}</section>`;
}
function calendarView() {
  const hubTabs=`<div class="uep-hub-tabs calendar-hub-tabs"><button class="${calendarHubMode==="schedule"?"active":""}" data-calendar-hub="schedule"><b>학교일정</b><small>월간·주간 일정</small></button><button class="${calendarHubMode==="timetable"?"active":""}" data-calendar-hub="timetable"><b>시간표</b><small>개인·학급·학생·교사</small></button></div>`;
  if(calendarHubMode==="timetable") return `<div class="module-page calendar-timetable-hub">${hubTabs}${timetableView()}</div>`;
  const anchor=new Date(`${calendarSelectedDate||dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
  return `<div class="module-page calendar-page-v654 school-schedule-page">${hubTabs}<div class="calendar-page-toolbar"><div><small>SCHOOL SCHEDULE · 데이터처리시트 41_학교캘린더</small><h3>학교일정</h3><p>월간계획을 기본으로 확인하고 월간·주간 보기를 자유롭게 전환합니다.</p></div><div><button class="btn secondary" data-calendar-today>${state.calendarMode==="week"?"이번 주":"오늘"}</button><button class="btn primary" data-reference="calendar-add">＋ 개인 일정</button></div></div><div class="school-schedule-summary"><b>${anchor.getFullYear()}년 ${anchor.getMonth()+1}월</b><span>선택일 ${anchor.getMonth()+1}/${anchor.getDate()} · ${schoolEventsForDate(dateKey(anchor)).length}건</span></div><div class="card calendar-page-card school-schedule-card">${calendarMarkup(true)}<div class="widget-source-footer dashboard-source">출처: 데이터처리시트 41_학교캘린더 · 대시보드 월간/주간과 동일 DB</div></div></div>`;

function inputCenterBatchStudents(){
  const students=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).filter(s=>!String(s.status||s.enrollmentStatus||'').includes('전출'));
  const scope=inputCenterConfig.batchScope||'학급';
  if(scope==='학급') return students.filter(s=>String(s.grade||String(s.studentNo||'').slice(0,1))===String(inputCenterConfig.batchGrade||'1') && String(s.className||classNumberOf(s)||'').replace(/반/g,'')===String(inputCenterConfig.batchClass||'1'));
  if(scope==='학년전체') return students.filter(s=>String(s.grade||String(s.studentNo||'').slice(0,1))===String(inputCenterConfig.batchGrade||'1'));
  if(scope==='개별') return students.filter(s=>String(s.studentNo||'')===String(inputCenterConfig.batchStudentNo||'').trim());
  return students;
}
function inputCenterBatchExpectedCount(){
  const scope=inputCenterConfig.batchScope||'학급';
  if(scope==='사용자지정') return Math.max(0,Number(inputCenterConfig.expectedCount||0));
  return inputCenterBatchStudents().length;
}

function subjectsView() {
  return `<div class="module-page">${toolbar("학생·희망과목·오류 검색", "신청 입력")}<div class="data-card"><div class="data-head student-grid"><span>학번</span><span>학생</span><span>1차 신청</span><span>상담</span><span>상태</span></div>${samples.students.map((r, i) => `<div class="data-row student-grid"><b>${r[0]}</b><span>${r[1]}</span><span>${i === 2 ? "미신청" : "완료"}</span><span>${i === 2 ? "필요" : "-"}</span><span class="state ${i === 2 ? "warn" : ""}">${i === 2 ? "확인 필요" : "정상"}</span></div>`).join("")}</div></div>`;
}
function schoolScheduleTimetableMarkup(){
  const anchor=new Date(`${calendarSelectedDate||dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
  const monday=new Date(anchor); monday.setDate(anchor.getDate()-((anchor.getDay()+6)%7));
  const days=Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>{const d=new Date(monday);d.setDate(monday.getDate()+i);return d;});
  const dayEvents=days.map(d=>schoolEventsForDate(dateKey(d)).filter(calendarEventMatchesFilter).sort((a,b)=>String(a.time||"99:99").localeCompare(String(b.time||"99:99"))));
  const maxRows=Math.max(1,...dayEvents.map(rows=>rows.length));
  const rows=Array.from({length:maxRows},(_,rowIndex)=>`<div class="school-schedule-row"><div class="school-schedule-slot">${rowIndex+1}</div>${dayEvents.map(events=>{const event=events[rowIndex];if(!event)return '<div class="school-schedule-cell empty"><span>—</span></div>';const kind=calendarEventVisualKind(event);return `<button class="school-schedule-cell kind-${kind} ${calendarEventIsPending(event)?"pending":""}" data-school-event="${escapeHtml(event.id)}"><time>${escapeHtml(event.time||"종일")}</time><b>${escapeHtml(calendarShortTitle(calendarDisplayTitle(event),26))}</b><small>${escapeHtml([event.target,event.place].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")||event.owner||"학교일정")}</small></button>`;}).join("")}</div>`).join("");
  return `<section class="school-schedule-timetable"><div class="school-schedule-head"><div class="school-schedule-corner">순서</div>${days.map(d=>`<button data-calendar-date="${dateKey(d)}" class="${dateKey(d)===dateKey(today)?"today":""}"><b>${["일","월","화","수","목","금","토"][d.getDay()]}</b><span>${d.getMonth()+1}/${d.getDate()}</span><em>${schoolEventsForDate(dateKey(d)).length}건</em></button>`).join("")}</div>${rows}</section>`;
}
function calendarView() {
  const hubTabs=`<div class="uep-hub-tabs calendar-hub-tabs"><button class="${calendarHubMode==="schedule"?"active":""}" data-calendar-hub="schedule"><b>학교일정</b><small>월간·주간 일정</small></button><button class="${calendarHubMode==="timetable"?"active":""}" data-calendar-hub="timetable"><b>시간표</b><small>개인·학급·학생·교사</small></button></div>`;
  if(calendarHubMode==="timetable") return `<div class="module-page calendar-timetable-hub">${hubTabs}${timetableView()}</div>`;
  const anchor=new Date(`${calendarSelectedDate||dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
  return `<div class="module-page calendar-page-v654 school-schedule-page">${hubTabs}<div class="calendar-page-toolbar"><div><small>SCHOOL SCHEDULE · 데이터처리시트 41_학교캘린더</small><h3>학교일정</h3><p>월간계획을 기본으로 확인하고 월간·주간 보기를 자유롭게 전환합니다.</p></div><div><button class="btn secondary" data-calendar-today>${state.calendarMode==="week"?"이번 주":"오늘"}</button><button class="btn primary" data-reference="calendar-add">＋ 개인 일정</button></div></div><div class="school-schedule-summary"><b>${anchor.getFullYear()}년 ${anchor.getMonth()+1}월</b><span>선택일 ${anchor.getMonth()+1}/${anchor.getDate()} · ${schoolEventsForDate(dateKey(anchor)).length}건</span></div><div class="card calendar-page-card school-schedule-card">${calendarMarkup(true)}<div class="widget-source-footer dashboard-source">출처: 데이터처리시트 41_학교캘린더 · 대시보드 월간/주간과 동일 DB</div></div></div>`;
}

function inputCenterBatchStudents(){
  const students=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).filter(s=>!String(s.status||s.enrollmentStatus||'').includes('전출'));
  const scope=inputCenterConfig.batchScope||'학급';
  if(scope==='학급') return students.filter(s=>String(s.grade||String(s.studentNo||'').slice(0,1))===String(inputCenterConfig.batchGrade||'1') && String(s.className||classNumberOf(s)||'').replace(/반/g,'')===String(inputCenterConfig.batchClass||'1'));
  if(scope==='학년전체') return students.filter(s=>String(s.grade||String(s.studentNo||'').slice(0,1))===String(inputCenterConfig.batchGrade||'1'));
  if(scope==='개별') return students.filter(s=>String(s.studentNo||'')===String(inputCenterConfig.batchStudentNo||'').trim());
  return students;
}
function inputCenterBatchExpectedCount(){
  const scope=inputCenterConfig.batchScope||'학급';
  if(scope==='사용자지정') return Math.max(0,Number(inputCenterConfig.expectedCount||0));
  return inputCenterBatchStudents().length;
}

  const students=inputCenterBatchStudents();
  const hay=`${fileName||''}\n${textValue||''}`;
  const numbers=[...hay.matchAll(/(?:^|[^0-9])([123][0-

function openNightAttendanceDetail(studentKey, day, slot) {
  const students = readonlyCache?.students || [];
  const student = students.find(item => attendanceStudentKey(item) === studentKey) || {};
  const rows = readonlyCache?.nightAttendance || readonlyCache?.attendance || [];
  const row = rows.find(item => item.date === day && nightSlotName(item) === slot && (attendanceStudentKey(item) === studentKey || String(item.studentNo||"") === String(student.studentNo||"")));
  openDrawer(`${student.name || "학생"} · ${day} ${slot}`);
  $("#drawerTitle")?.classList.add("privacy-sensitive-text");
  $("#drawerBody").innerHTML = `<div class="history-summary positive"><span><small>학번</small><b class="privacy-sensitive-text">${escapeHtml(student.studentNo||"-")}</b></span><span><small>학급</small><b>${escapeHtml(student.className||"-")}반</b></span><span><small>일자</small><b>${escapeHtml(day)}</b></span><span><small>시간</small><b>${escapeHtml(slot)}</b></span></div><div class="drawer-section"><h4>야자출결 상세</h4><dl class="detail-list"><dt>결과</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.result||"출석 기록 없음")}</dd><dt>소속·유형</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.affiliation||row?.type||"일반 야자")}</dd><dt>상세</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.detail||"-")}</dd><dt>원본 시간</dt><dd>${escapeHtml(row?.time||row?.period||slot)}</dd></dl></div><p class="drawer-note">방과후·야간심화·추가 프로그램 상태는 해당 프로그램 상세 화면으로 바로 연결됩니다.</p>`;
}
